### [108] [Designing tight frames for quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27247)
*Luis Quezada*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文探索了利用表示论和群论构造谐振紧框架（harmonic tight frames）作为量子测量的方法，给出了基于阿贝尔群结构定理的谐振框架特征化、可分性充要条件、最大纠缠条件，并设计了可在量子计算机上实现循环群谐振框架为POVM的量子电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量是量子计算与量子信息中的核心问题。谐振紧框架作为一种特殊的量子测量，具有对称性和结构化的优点，但如何系统性地构造、分析其可分性/纠缠性质，并在实际量子计算机上实现这些测量，仍然是一个值得深入探索的问题。

Method: 本文采用表示论作为主要工具，从不可约表示及它们满足的关系出发构造群框架；聚焦于最简单的一类群——阿贝尔群，利用阿贝尔群分类定理和不可约表示的特征标来系统地分析谐振框架。在量子力学语境下，将框架元素解释为纯态，研究可分性的充要条件；将框架视为POVM，借助Naimark定理设计量子计算机上的测量方案。最终利用阿贝尔群结构定理给出谐振框架的特征化，研究双体中最大纠缠的条件，并设计由Fourier矩阵和置换矩阵构成的量子电路来实现循环群对应的谐振框架POVM。

Result: 获得了谐振框架的可分性充要条件（基于阿贝尔群结构定理）；得到了双体谐振框架态最大纠缠对子系统维数的必要条件；构造了利用Fourier矩阵和置换矩阵实现循环群谐振框架POVM的量子电路，并给出了在量子计算机上的简单应用示例。

Conclusion: 本文构建了一套从表示论到量子电路实现的完整理论框架，为谐振框架作为量子测量的实现提供了系统性方案。未来研究可进一步优化电路设计，并将该方法推广到所有谐振框架的情形。

Abstract: The aim of this thesis is to explore the implementation of a special kind of quantum measurements, so called harmonic tight frames. To achieve this goal, representation theory is addressed, emphasizing its construction from irreducible representations and the relations they satisfy. These tools simplify the study due to the existence of symmetries, leading to the concept of group frames, defined as orbits under the unitary action of a group and characterized by their symmetry groups. Among the various groups, the simplest are the abelian ones, giving rise to harmonic frames, which are analyzed through the classification theorem of abelian groups and the characters of their irreducible representations. In the quantum mechanics context, the frame elements can be interpreted as pure states of a system. Therefore, the necessary and sufficient conditions for separability are explored, as separable states are easier to implement due to their local nature. Alternatively, when viewing frames as measurements, the concept of POVMs and Naimark's theorem are studied as key tools for designing measurements in quantum computers. Using this knowledge, a characterization of the harmonic frames is given from the abelian group structure theorem, which allows obtaining a necessary and sufficient condition for their separability. The conditions of maximum entanglement of these states for bipartite systems are also studied, obtaining a necessary condition on the dimensions of the subsystems. Finally, a quantum circuit is obtained that allows implementing the harmonic frames associated to cyclic groups as POVMs using a Fourier matrix and a permutation matrix. We conclude by giving simple examples where the results are applied to quantum computers and proposing future avenues of research that could serve to improve the circuit design and extend it to the case of all harmonic frames.

</details>


### [109] [Geometry-Induced Domain-Wall Pinning and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ Asymmetry in Nominally One-Dimensional Rydberg Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27358)
*Elijah Pelofske,Frank Barrows,Cristiano Nisoli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在QuEra的Aquila中性里德伯原子量子计算机上，对两种一维几何构型（33原子闭合三角形和47原子含空缺的正方形）执行准绝热演化实验，发现与纯1D Ising模型预测不符的效应，包括由于范德瓦尔斯相互作用与里德伯封锁之间耦合导致的畴壁钉扎和Z2对称性破缺，特别是原子空缺周围和正方形顶点的原子强烈偏好里德伯态，从而在空缺上形成有效的铁磁键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望利用里德伯原子量子计算平台Aquila来研究一维量子伊辛模型的物理行为，并探索该硬件特有的几何约束（原子限制在2D平面）如何影响纯1D模型的物理现象，揭示超出理想模型预测的新效应。

Method: 在QuEra的Aquila中性里德伯原子量子计算机上执行准绝热演化实验，对两种几何构型（33原子闭合三角形和47原子含空缺的正方形）进行硬件微调，以平衡接近零净磁化附近的测量统计。利用该类处理器将原子编程为2D平面几何的限制，研究几何形状和原子空缺对量子动力学的影响。

Result: 观察到纯1D Ising模型未预测的效应：domain walls（畴壁）被钉扎，且由于范德瓦尔斯相互作用与里德伯封锁之间的相互作用导致Z2对称性破缺。此外，原子空缺周围的原子和正方形构型顶点的原子强烈偏好里德伯态，使得有效模型在空缺位置形成一条铁磁键。

Conclusion: 尽管硬件被编程为1D模型，但处理器的2D平面几何限制引入了超越纯1D Ising模型的物理效应。原子空缺和几何顶点的存在会导致里德伯态偏好和有效的铁磁相互作用，这需要在解释实验数据时考虑硬件特有的几何效应。

Abstract: We perform experiments on QuEra's neutral-Rydberg-atom Aquila quantum computer, using quasi-adiabatic evolution on 1-dimensional models. We use hardware fine-tuning to balance the measurement statistics close to zero net magnetization, in two different geometric outlines: one closed triangular model with 33 atoms, and one 47-atom square with an engineered atom vacancy. The nature of this processor restricts the geometry of the atoms that can be programmed to a 2D plane. We report effects not predicted by the pure 1D Ising model, such as pinning of domain walls and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry breaking due to the interplay between van der Waals interactions and the Rydberg blockade. In particular, atoms around the atom vacancy and at the vertices of the 1-dimensional square-outline model strongly prefer the Rydberg state, leading to the effective model having a ferromagnetic bond across the vacancy.

</details>


### [110] [Is the H Atom Surrounded by A Cloud of Virtual Quanta Due to the Lamb Shift?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27257)
*G. Jordan Maclay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Feynman和Power的框架，将Lamb位移视为氢原子存在导致真空能量密度变化的结果，利用群论方法计算每个频率对Lamb位移的贡献，从而推导出各频率真空能量在氢原子周围的空间分布范围。基态氢原子被一个正真空能量区域包围，对低频辐射该区域可延伸至宏观尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lamb位移是原子物理学中最基本的相互作用之一，源自氢原子与量子真空电磁涨落的相互作用。虽然能量位移已有多种计算方法，但此前缺乏对每个频率真空涨落贡献对应的空间区域大小的系统描述。作者旨在定量刻画Lamb位移所涉及的真空能量在空间中的分布范围。

Method: 利用Feynman和Power证明的结论（Lamb位移等于氢原子存在引起折射率变化所导致的真空能量变化），结合群论方法计算每个频率对Lamb位移的贡献，从而推导出每个频率的真空涨落对应的能量空间区域尺寸。

Result: 基态氢原子周围存在一个延伸到原子以外的正真空能量区域，可描述为虚拟量子的稳态云。对于能量E=ℏω小于1 eV的涨落，正能量区域半径约为14.4/E埃，即半径等于(α/2π)λ。对长波长涨落，该区域具有宏观尺寸。能量-时间不确定性关系给出的最大可能半径比该值大1/(4α)倍。

Conclusion: Lamb位移对应的真空能量在每个频率下具有特定的空间扩展范围，低频真空涨落对应宏观尺度的正能量区域，这为理解Lamb位移的物理本质以及真空涨落与物质相互作用的空间结构提供了新的视角。

Abstract: \abstract{The Lamb shift, one of the most fundamental interactions in atomic physics, arises from the interaction of H atoms with the electromagnetic fluctuations of the quantum vacuum. The energy shift has been computed in a variety of ways. The energy shift, as Feynman and Power demonstrated, equals the change in the vacuum energy in the volume containing the H atoms due to the change in the index of refraction arising from the presence of the H atoms. By using this result and a group theoretical calculation of the contribution to the Lamb shift from each frequency of the vacuum fluctuations, we can obtain an expression for the size of the region of vacuum energy for each frequency \texorpdfstring{$ω$}{ω} around the H atom due to the Lamb shift. The ground state atom is surrounded by a region of positive vacuum energy that extends well beyond the atom for low frequencies. This region can be described as a steady state cloud of virtual quanta. For energies \texorpdfstring{$E=\hbarω$}{E=hbar omega} eV less than 1 eV, the radius of the positive energy region is approximately 14. 4/E Angstroms. For a vacuum fluctuation of wavelength \texorpdfstring{$λ$}{lambda} the radius is \texorpdfstring{$(α/2π)λ$}{(alpha/2pi) lambda}. Thus, for long wavelengths, the region has macroscopic dimensions. The energy-time Uncertainty Relation predicts a maximum possible radius that is larger than this by a factor of \texorpdfstring{$1/ 4α$}{1/(4 alpha)}.}
  \keyword{Bethe; radiative shift; shift spectral density; spectral volume; vacuum fluctuations; vacuum field; Lamb shift; QED; energy field, renormalization, zero point fluctuations; hydrogen atom}.

</details>


### [111] [Noise-resilient and Scalable Quantum Error Correction for Nuclear Spin Qubits in Silicon with Electron Shuttling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27527)
*Wayne M. Witzel,Matthew D. Grace,Dwight R. Luhman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出电子对干涉测量（EPI）协议，利用量子点阵列中的等电子核自旋量子比特，通过单重态/三重态编码电子实现对核自旋奇偶性的相干读取，并结合全局NMR实现通用量子计算，对电荷噪声具有极低敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅中的核自旋量子比特与环境隔离良好，具有极长的寿命和低噪声敏感性，但这也意味着控制和测量非常困难。作者旨在克服这一挑战，同时保持对噪声的鲁棒性。

Method: EPI协议在量子点阵列中实现：将一对电子初始化到单重态基态，分离并穿梭至含有核自旋量子比特的量子点中，将核的奇偶性相干转移到可测量的单重态/三重态编码电子态上。利用全局核磁共振（NMR）改变基矢并实现动力学去耦（DD），结合选择性超精细诱导的Z_π旋转构成完整门集。

Result: 证明了能够将核自旋的奇偶性相干转移到可测量的电子态上；门集对Calderbank-Shor-Steane（CSS）量子纠错是完整的；对电荷噪声具有极低敏感性；对DC和AC磁场不均匀性的敏感性强烈依赖其相对强度。

Conclusion: EPI协议为克服硅基核自旋量子比特控制与测量困难提供了可行方案，在保持长寿命优势的同时实现了鲁棒、通用的量子计算能力。

Abstract: Nuclear spin qubits in silicon are well-isolated from their environment. Consequently, they have very long lifetimes and low sensitivity to noise, but this also suggests that control and measurement is challenging. We introduce electron pair interferometry (EPI), a protocol to overcome this challenge and maintain robustness to noise. EPI is implemented using an array of quantum dots with isoelectronic nuclear spin qubits located in the dots. A pair of electrons are initialized into a singlet ground state, split apart, and shuttled to the dots containing nuclear spin qubits. We show it is possible to coherently transfer the parity of the nuclei onto the measurable state of singlet/triplet-encoded electrons. Global nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) can be used to change bases and implement dynamical decoupling (DD). Combined with selective hyperfine-induced $Z_π$ rotations, our gate set is complete for universal quantum computation, tailored to Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) quantum error correction, and robust to noise. We discuss very low sensitivity to charge noise and study the sensitivity to both DC and AC magnetic field inhomogeneity which depends strongly on their relative strengths.

</details>


### [112] [LLM-Guided Initialization for Accelerated Hybrid Quantum-Classical Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27262)
*Riza Alaudin Syah,Irwan Alnarus Kautsar,Haza Nuzly Bin Abdull Hamed*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了AdaInit（自适应初始化）方法，该方法利用大型语言模型为量子神经网络提出初始参数。在DMR-IR乳腺X光数据集上的二分类任务中，AdaInit相比随机初始化将梯度方差提升14.6倍，收敛速度提升160倍，同时保持61.4%的分类准确率，并通过理论分析与GPU加速模拟验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法常遭遇贫瘠高原（barren plateaus）问题，即成本函数梯度随电路深度增加而急剧衰减，严重削弱参数化量子电路的可训练性。本文旨在探索一种低开销的初始化策略，以缓解这一问题并在实际应用场景中验证其可行性。

Method: 研究采用单次查询的简化AdaInit变体，利用大语言模型（LLM）生成量子神经网络的初始参数；同时结合NVIDIA CUDA-Q平台的GPU加速模拟，在DMR-IR乳腺X光数据集上执行二分类任务。文中还提供了基于参数化电路景观几何的理论分析，并从经验上验证LLM引导的初始化使优化器进入可训练的参数区域。

Result: AdaInit在初始化时比随机初始化高出14.6倍的梯度方差（0.0095 vs 0.0006），收敛速度提升160倍（1.1秒 vs 176秒），分类准确率保持相同的61.4%。此外，结果表明单次LLM查询即可获得信息量充足的参数，无需迭代细化。

Conclusion: 研究在医学影像场景中验证了AdaInit的有效性，证明单次LLM查询便能生成具有信息量的初始化参数，为改善变分量子算法的可训练性提供了低开销的路径，并展示了其与GPU加速量子后端的兼容性以实现实际加速。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms often encounter barren plateaus, where cost gradients decay rapidly with increasing circuit depth, undermining the trainability of parameterized quantum circuits. This paper evaluates AdaInit (Adaptive Initialization), proposed by Zhuang and Cunningham, which uses large language models to propose initial parameters for quantum neural networks. We study a simplified single-query AdaInit variant paired with GPU-accelerated simulation in NVIDIA CUDA-Q and apply it to binary classification on the DMR-IR mammography dataset. AdaInit delivers 14.6 times higher gradient variance at initialization than random initialization (0.0095 vs. 0.0006), producing 160 times faster convergence (1.1s vs. 176 s) while maintaining the same classification accuracy of 61.4 percent. We provide theoretical analysis grounded in the geometry of parameterized circuit landscapes and show empirically that LLM-guided initialization places the optimizer in trainable regions of parameter space. Beyond performance, our results indicate that a single LLM query can yield informative parameters without iterative refinement, suggesting a low-overhead path to improved trainability. The findings validate AdaInit in a medical imaging setting and demonstrate its compatibility with GPU-accelerated quantum backends for practical speedups.

</details>


### [113] [Quantum State Discrimination With Stabilizer Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27339)
*Benjamin Stratton,Santiago Zamora,Rafael Chaves*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在稳定子电路测量约束下的量子态最小错误判别问题。研究表明：给定任意稳定子辅助态时，固定稳定子电路不提供额外判别能力，而自适应电路可提供；针对单量子比特系统，推导出了两种电路类在有任意（非稳定子）辅助态时成功概率的解析表达式；此外，将非稳定子性直接引入测量算子进行判别，通过半定规划优化并推导出在稳定子和Helstrom极限之间插值的解析界；最后展示了在量子随机访问码和有限魔法态下酉合成保真度界的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态判别是量子信息科学中刻画可区分性的核心任务，其成功概率同时依赖于待判别量子态和允许的测量方式。为了理解物理上可行的测量约束（如稳定子电路）对判别能力的影响，作者希望系统研究不同电路类别下量子态判别的最优成功概率及其性能差距。

Method: 采用理论分析与解析推导相结合的方法：1）利用稳定子电路的结构特性证明固定电路对任意稳定子辅助态无额外判别力；2）针对单比特系统分别推导固定和自适应稳定子电路在任意辅助态下的最小错误判别成功概率解析表达式；3）将非稳定子性（magic资源）引入测量算子，将优化问题表述为半定规划（SDP），并推导稳定子极限与Helstrom极限之间插值的解析界。

Result: 1）固定稳定子电路在给定稳定子辅助态时不提升判别能力，而自适应电路可以；2）单比特系统中，固定与自适应电路的性能差距在任意（非稳定子）辅助态下依然存在；3）构建了包含非稳定子性的测量算子的半定规划，给出了介于稳定子极限和Helstrom极限之间的解析插值界；4）将结果应用于量子随机访问码和有限魔法态下的酉合成保真度界。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了稳定子电路测量约束下量子态判别的能力边界，揭示了固定与自适应电路在判别能力上的本质差异，并为非稳定子资源（magic资源）在量子态判别中的作用提供了定量理解，同时展示了这些理论结果在量子信息处理任务中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: The task of quantum state discrimination provides an operational characterization of distinguishability and plays a central role in quantum information science. Since the achievable success probability in quantum state discrimination depends both on the states being discriminated and on the allowed measurements, it is natural to study discrimination under physically motivated measurement constraints. Here, we investigate minimum-error quantum state discrimination under measurements implementable by both fixed and adaptive stabilizer circuits. Given arbitrary stabilizer-state ancillas, we show that fixed stabilizer circuits provide no additional discrimination power, whereas adaptive circuits do. Then, focusing on single-qubit systems, we derive analytical expressions for the success probability across both circuit classes when supplied with arbitrary (non-stabilizer) ancillas, showing that the performance gap between fixed and adaptive circuits persists. We further consider discrimination with non-stabilizerness incorporated directly into the measurement operators. Here, the optimization is formulated as a semidefinite program, and analytical bounds interpolating between the stabilizer and Helstrom limits for qubits are derived. Finally, we illustrate applications in quantum random access codes and bounds on unitary synthesis fidelity with a finite number of magic states.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum Magnonics: Quantum States Generation and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27771)
*Zi-Xu Lu,Xuan Zuo,Xin-Lei Hei,Gang Liu,Zeng-Xing Liu,Qi Guo,Peng-Bo Li,Jie Li*

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于铁磁材料（如钇铁石榴石）中磁子的混合量子系统在最近十年的实验与理论进展，重点涵盖磁子量子态的生成及其在宏观量子研究、量子信息科学、量子传感、磁子量子器件和暗物质探测等领域的广泛应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁子混合系统在过去十年取得了显著发展（包括与微波光子、光学光子、超导量子比特、声子、自旋以及铁磁体质心运动的耦合），但该领域需要一个系统性的综述来梳理磁子量子态的产生与应用的全面进展。

Method: 本文首先介绍腔磁子学中的代表性强耦合实验，因为强耦合是实现磁子相干量子控制和制备磁子量子态的前提；随后综述一系列用于产生各类磁子量子态（如Fock态、猫态、压缩态和纠缠态）的方案协议，并讨论这些量子态的潜在应用领域。

Result: 该综述系统性地总结了磁子量子态的产生方案（Fock、猫态、压缩态、纠缠态等）及其在宏观量子研究、量子信息科学、量子传感、磁子量子器件、暗物质探测等多个领域的潜在应用前景。

Conclusion: 作者对该综述进行了总结，并对未来量子磁子学的研究方向给出了展望。

Abstract: Hybrid systems based on magnons in ferromagnetic materials, such as yttrium iron garnet, have achieved remarkable development in the last decade. These include the coupling of magnons to microwave and optical photons, superconducting qubits, phonons, spins, the center-of-mass motion of a ferromagnet, etc. Here, we review both the experimental and theoretical progress in this field, focusing on the generation of magnonic quantum states and their applications in a broad range of fields. Since the strong coupling is a prerequisite for achieving coherent quantum control of magnons and preparing magnonic quantum states, we start by introducing representative strong-coupling experiments in cavity magnonics, then review a series of protocols for creating various magnonic quantum states, such as Fock, cat, squeezed, and entangled states, and discuss their potential applications in macroscopic quantum studies, quantum information science, quantum sensing, magnonic quantum devices, dark matter detection, and so on. Finally, we summarize the review and give an outlook for the future study of quantum magnonics.

</details>


### [115] [Bayesian Networks of Density Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27876)
*Steffen Lauritzen,Piotr Zwiernik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了有向无环图（DAG）上量子贝叶斯网络的两个构造方案：内在构造（从联合态出发研究条件独立性质）和外在构造（按DAG顺序从局部量子核逐步组装态）。证明了内在构造中有序、局部和全局有向马尔可夫性质的等价性及其熵、递归因子化和对数刻画；外在构造总能得到归一化态并能从条件概率恢复每个核，但谷-父母边缘态不能恢复同一核（三比特反例展示）。证明拓扑序独立的充分必要条件恰为传递DAG；每个正定态和DAG关联的对数候选是子归一化的，且迹为一的候选是有向马尔可夫态，两者在DAG马尔可夫等价下不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典贝叶斯网络在有向无环图上的条件独立性质已被广泛研究，但其量子对应物缺乏系统性的理论框架。已有的量子贝叶斯网络构建方法在内在性质（从联合态出发的马尔可夫性质）和外在性质（从局部核组装态）之间存在张力，且拓扑序的选择是否影响构建结果尚未明确。本文旨在厘清这两种构造方案的数学结构，并给出严格的等价性与不变性刻画。

Method: 本文在有限维张量积希尔伯特空间上对正定密度算子进行形式化分析。内在构造利用有向马尔可夫性质（有序、局部、全局三种形式），通过条件独立性、熵等式、递归因子化和对数表示进行刻画，并证明这些刻画之间的等价性。外在构造按DAG拓扑序将局部量子核依次组合为联合态。借助三比特量子实例展示核恢复的障碍，并通过传递DAG的结构特征分析拓扑序独立性的充要条件。最后构造一个与每个正定态和DAG关联的对数候选，证明其子归一化及马尔可夫态性质。

Result: （1）内在构造中有序、局部、全局有向马尔可夫性质等价，并与熵、递归因子化和对数刻画互为充要条件；（2）外在构造总给出归一化态，可从整体条件恢复每个核，但一般不能从顶点-父母边缘态恢复同一核（三比特反例证实）；（3）拓扑序独立性成立的充要条件是DAG为传递的；（4）每个正定态和DAG的对数候选是子归一化的，迹为一时恰为有向马尔可夫态；（5）候选态和超额全局信息在DAG马尔可夫等价下均不变。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了量子贝叶斯网络在DAG上的内在与外在两种构造的数学理论。内在构造提供了完整的马尔可夫性质刻画体系，外在构造揭示了局部核组装与整体条件恢复之间的本质障碍。传递DAG刻画了拓扑序独立性的边界，而对数候选结构为量子有向马尔可夫态提供了统一的不变构造。这些结果对量子因果推断、量子信息网络和量子机器学习中的模型选择具有理论支撑价值。

Abstract: We study quantum analogues of Bayesian networks on a directed acyclic graph (DAG), distinguishing two constructions for positive definite density operators on finite-dimensional tensor-product Hilbert spaces. The intrinsic construction starts from a joint state and its conditional-independence properties. The extrinsic construction assembles a state sequentially from prescribed local quantum kernels, following an ordering compatible with the arrows of the DAG. For the intrinsic construction, we prove the equivalence of the ordered, local, and global directed Markov properties, together with entropy, recursive-factorization, and logarithmic characterizations. The extrinsic construction always gives a normalized state and recovers each kernel as a conditional on all preceding systems. The same kernel, however, need not be recovered from the marginal on the vertex and its parents; a three-qubit example exhibits this obstruction. We prove that independence of the chosen topological ordering is sufficient exactly for transitive DAGs: every order-invariant kernel family then yields an intrinsically directed Markov state. Finally, we associate a logarithmic candidate with every positive definite state and DAG, prove that it is subnormalized, and show that the trace-one candidate is a directed Markov state. Both the candidate and the excess global information are invariant under DAG Markov equivalence.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum vs Classical Erasure: Equal Bounds but Unequal Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27341)
*Jan Neuser,Jake Xuereb,Pharnam Bakhshinezhad,Marcus Huber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对经典与量子系统中的有限资源信息擦除提供了统一的第一性原理描述，证明在理想极限下两者擦除成本相同，但在实际实现中量子系统需要更多控制、更大的能隙和更长的操作时间，而经典协议可在更弱约束下实现可比的质量擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landauer边界量化了擦除单个比特信息所需的最小耗散，但该边界仅在无限资源的理想极限下才饱和。此前缺乏对有限资源条件下经典与量子系统擦除的统一描述，且对两者在实际实现中差异的定量理解不足。

Method: 作者提供了经典与量子系统有限资源擦除的统一第一性原理描述，首先证明在理想化极限下量子与经典系统中比特擦除成本相同，随后推导出经典协议与量子协议在擦除质量比较下的约束差异，并通过权衡关系揭示两者的实际实现条件差别。

Result: 经典和量子系统在理想极限下具有相同的擦除成本，但量子系统的实际实现需要更强的控制条件、更大的可访问能隙和更长的操作时间；经典协议在远更弱的约束下即能达到与量子协议相当的擦除质量。这些发现解释了实际擦除方案达不到Landauer边界的原因，并表明经典系统具有若干基础热力学优势。

Conclusion: 本文从第一性原理统一解释了经典与量子有限资源擦除的差异，阐明了Landauer边界无法饱和的根本原因，同时揭示了经典系统在信息擦除上的基础热力学优势。

Abstract: Irreversibility has a fundamental thermodynamic cost, erasing information inevitably generates heat. This connection is quantified by the Landauer bound, which gives the minimum dissipation needed to erase a single bit of information. While this bound applies in both classical and quantum settings, it is saturated only in idealised limits of infinite resources. Here, we provide a unified first principles description of finite-resource erasure in both classical and quantum systems. We begin by proving the communal folklore that in the idealised regime the erasure cost of a bit encoded in a quantum or classical system is the same. Despite this, we show that their practical implementation differs substantially: achieving comparable erasure quality in quantum systems requires more control, larger accessible energy gaps and longer operation times. Classical protocols can achieve the erasure of a comparable quantum protocol under far weaker constraints which we expose in trade-off relations. Our results explain why practical erasure schemes fall short of Landauer's bound and show that classical systems enjoy several fundamental thermodynamic advantages.

</details>


### [117] [Bridging continuous control and Floquet driving for charging many-body spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27985)
*Sebastián V. Romero,Xi Chen,Yue Ban*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了基于自旋系统的量子电池研究进展，涵盖充电与做功提取协议、外部因素对性能的影响、连续驱动与周期驱动自旋链在量子能量存储中的联系，以及当前及近期量子技术中的实验实现与扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息和量子热力学的最新进展推动了对微观尺度能量存储的理解，量子电池作为传统宏观化学电池的概念性替代方案，能够利用量子相干性、关联和多体动力学，因此有必要系统梳理该领域的已有协议、外部因素影响及可扩展性，为该方向的发展提供全景式参考。

Method: 作者对已建立的自旋基量子电池进行导航式综述，梳理现有充电和做功提取协议，分析外部因素对性能的影响；借鉴Floquet工程化方案，建立连续驱动与周期驱动自旋链作为量子能量存储平台之间的关联；最后调查实验实现与实验提案，评估其可实施性和可扩展性。

Result: 文章建立起连续驱动与周期驱动自旋链之间的理论联系，并系统评估了自旋基量子电池在现有和近期量子技术中的实验可行性与可扩展性，为该领域提供了全面的研究图谱。

Conclusion: 自旋基量子电池在微观能量存储方面展现出显著潜力，通过将驱动协议与实验平台相结合，该领域正朝着实现可扩展、可实施的量子能量存储装置方向稳步推进。

Abstract: Recent advances in quantum information and quantum thermodynamics have reshaped the understanding of energy storage at the microscopic scale, paving the way toward protocols for storing and transferring energy in quantum devices. These systems, known as quantum batteries, offer a conceptual alternative to conventional macroscopic chemical batteries by exploiting quantum coherence, correlations, and many-body dynamics. By navigating the landscape of established spin-based quantum batteries, we review existing charging and work-extraction protocols, as well as the impact of external factors on their performance. Motivated by Floquet-engineered proposals, we further establish a connection between continuously and periodically driven spin chains as platforms for quantum energy storage. Finally, we survey experimental realizations and proposals, highlighting their implementability and scalability in current and near-term quantum technologies.

</details>


### [118] [Restrictions on non-Clifford fault tolerance and ruling out beyond-SQL quantum metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27342)
*Constantin Cedillo Vayson de Pradenne,Ishaan Kannan,Harald Putterman,Jordan Cotler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了对横向量子门作用的严格限制，并将其应用于量子度量学。对于距离d≥3的稳定子码，若支持Clifford层级第D阶的横向逻辑作用，则每个稳定子生成集必须包含权重至少为2^D的校验子；r级联实现满足r≤⌊log₂n/D⌋，排除了超越SQL度量中的级联。同时证明横向小角度单比特旋转只能在具有高权重不可约稳定子的码上产生非平凡逻辑作用，导致综合征提取所需的校验子权重随n发散。最后给出一个不依赖量子Cramér-Rao界假设的更广泛度规无-go定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子度量学有望在标准量子极限(SQL)上实现二次加速，但信号对齐噪声被认为会阻碍这种优势。一种可能的绕过路径是将传感器编码在量子码中，使物理信号作为逻辑门横向作用。理解横向非Clifford门的限制对量子度量学和容错量子计算都至关重要。

Method: 作者首先证明，对于距离d≥3的稳定子码，若支持Clifford层级第D阶的横向逻辑作用，则每个稳定子生成集必须包含权重至少2^D的校验子，并由此推导出r级联实现满足r≤⌊log₂n/D⌋。其次，证明横向单比特小角度θ旋转只能在包含权重Ω(1/(n|θ|²))不可约稳定子的码上产生非平凡逻辑作用，低权重的单比特误差会与低于该权重的稳定子或逻辑Pauli算符交换，无法从低权重法向子测量中容错重构综合征。最后，提出一个更广泛的度量no-go定理，不依赖量子Cramér-Rao界的假设，证明常强度信号对齐噪声排除AC或DC传感中任何超越SQL的渐近优势。

Result: 对于支持Clifford层级第D阶横向逻辑作用的距离d≥3稳定子码，每个稳定子生成集必须包含权重至少2^D的校验子；r级联实现满足r≤1，排除级联提升的可能性。横向小角度单比特旋转在超越SQL度量中需要|θ|=o(n^{-1/2})，此时校验子权重随n发散。更广泛的no-go定理在包括有偏估计量、非稳定子或近似编码、量子记忆、中间测量和自适应控制等条件下排除了任何超越SQL的渐近优势。

Conclusion: 横向非Clifford门受到严格的码结构限制，这些限制从根本上排除了超越SQL的横向传感方案。信号对齐噪声即使在放宽所有常见假设的情况下，仍排除了量子度量学超越标准量子极限的任何渐近优势。

Abstract: Quantum metrology promises a quadratic speedup over the standard quantum limit (SQL), but signal-aligned noise is expected to preclude this advantage in realistic settings. A potential route around known no-go results is to encode the sensors in a quantum code where the physical signal acts transversally as a logical gate. Understanding restrictions on transversal non-Clifford gates is therefore central to both quantum metrology and fault-tolerant quantum computation. Here, we prove such restrictions and apply them to transversal sensing. For any stabilizer code of distance $d\ge 3$ supporting a transversal logical action in level $D$ of the Clifford hierarchy, every stabilizer generating set must contain a check of weight at least $2^D$. Moreover, any $r$-level concatenated realization satisfies $r\leq \lfloor \log_2 n/D\rfloor$, forcing $r=1$ and ruling out concatenation when applied to beyond-SQL metrology. We then show that transversal single-qubit rotations by a small angle $θ$ can only induce a nontrivial logical action on an $n$-qubit code if its checks include irreducible stabilizers of weight $Ω(1/(n|θ|^2))$. Here, many single-qubit errors commute with every stabilizer or logical Pauli below this weight and are only detected by a high-weight check, so their syndromes cannot be fault-tolerantly reconstructed from low-weight normalizer measurements. Since beyond-SQL transversal sensing requires $|θ| = o(n^{-1/2})$, the weight of checks required for syndrome extraction diverges with $n$. Finally, we prove a broader metrological no-go theorem that avoids the assumptions of the quantum Cramér-Rao bound: constant-strength signal-aligned noise rules out any asymptotic advantage over the SQL in AC or DC sensing, even with biased estimators, nonstabilizer or approximate encodings, quantum memory, intermediate measurements, or adaptive control.

</details>


### [119] [Scaling theory of decoherence in Dicke superradiance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28034)
*Nico S. Bassler,Julian Lyne,Javier Cuerda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了包含局域退相干和自发辐射的标度理论，揭示Dicke超辐射中多体相干性的存活取决于关联累积与退相干之间的竞争，并发现了全集体、部分集体和独立发射体三种标度区域，其中全集体区域的边界对应瞬态可观测量的连续相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多体相干性的存活条件——它依赖于关联累积与退相干之间的竞争。在Dicke超辐射中，集体发射会累积关联，产生与发射体数N的平方成正比的峰值强度，但局域退相干可能破坏这种N^2标度，因此需要系统研究退相干对集体标度行为的影响。

Method: 发展了一个包含局域退相和自发辐射的标度理论，通过分析不同参数区域下的标度行为，将系统划分为全集体、部分集体和独立发射体三个标度区域，并研究这些区域之间的相变边界。

Result: 发现了三种不同的标度区域：全集体区域、部分集体区域和独立发射体区域。全集体区域的边界被证明对应一个瞬态可观测量的连续相变。关键发现是局域退相干即使增加发射体数N，也可能阻止N^2标度的实现。

Conclusion: 局域退相干对多体相干性具有根本性的破坏作用，它可以在增加系统尺寸N的同时阻止N^2的超辐射标度，揭示了集体发射中关联累积与退相干竞争的平衡机制，为理解有限尺寸多体系统的相干性稳定性提供了标度图景。

Abstract: The survival of many-body coherence depends on the competition between correlation buildup and decoherence. In Dicke superradiance, collective emission builds up correlations, producing a peak intensity scaling as $N^2$ for $N$ emitters. We develop a scaling theory including local dephasing and spontaneous emission and obtain fully collective, partially collective, and independent-emitter scaling regimes. The boundary of the fully collective regime defines a continuous phase transition in a transient observable. Local decoherence can prevent $N^2$ scaling despite increasing $N$.

</details>


### [120] [Spectral gaps of ironed two-qubit gadgets matching the iSWAP gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28521)
*Yanying Liang,Haoran Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在完全图K_n（n≥5）上，所有KAK参数a=5/9的ironed two-qubit gadget与iSWAP gadget具有相同的二阶矩谱隙。核心方法是表示论局部化定理：相关的S_n不变算子的最大严格负特征值总出现在最高自旋SU(2)子空间中，并通过局部半正定分解将其余自旋扇区分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于ironed two-qubit gadget的谱隙性质存在一个Kong、Li和Liu提出的猜想，该猜想涉及此类gadget在完全图上的二阶矩谱隙是否与iSWAP gadget一致。验证这一猜想对于理解量子计算中gadget的谱性质具有理论意义。

Method: 作者运用表示论工具，核心是一个局部化定理，证明相关的S_n不变算子（由ironed two-qubit gadget诱导）的最大严格负特征值必然出现在SU(2)表示中最高自旋的子空间中。随后通过一个局部的半正定分解来分离除了两个最高自旋扇区之外的所有扇区，从而将问题简化为最高自旋扇区的分析。

Result: 证明了当KAK参数a=5/9时，ironed two-qubit gadget在完全图K_n（n≥5）上具有与iSWAP gadget相同的二阶矩谱隙，从而解决了Kong、Li和Liu提出的猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作通过表示论方法成功刻画了特定参数下ironed two-qubit gadget在完全图上的谱性质，结果表明该gadget与iSWAP在二阶矩谱隙意义上等价，为量子信息中gadget谱理论提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We prove that every ironed two-qubit gadget whose KAK-derived parameter satisfies $a=5/9$ has, on the complete graph $K_n$ with $n\geqslant 5$, the same second-moment spectral gap as the iSWAP gadget. The central step is a representation-theoretic localisation theorem: the largest strictly negative eigenvalue of the associated $\mathfrak S_n$-invariant operator always occurs in the highest-spin $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ summand. A local positive-semidefinite decomposition separates every spin sector except the two highest. This settles a conjecture of Kong, Li, and Liu.

</details>


### [121] [Area-Information Trade-Offs in Acceleration Radiation from Atoms Falling into Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27388)
*Yusef Maleki,Gustavo Valdivia-Mera,Carlos R. Ordonez,Horacio E. Camblong,Marlan O. Scully*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Horizon-brightened acceleration radiation (HBAR)信道中建立了信息处理的几何理论，通过辐射视界面积变化作为熵预算，推导出面积成本定律、Fano不等式可靠性约束和Fisher信息速度极限，确立了将黑洞热力学、信息几何与量子信息联系起来的bits-per-area原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将黑洞视界的热力学性质与信息处理理论建立定量联系。HBAR信道中辐射视界面积变化可提供熵预算，但缺乏严格的几何信息理论框架来刻画该信道中经典信息量、互信息以及可靠解码所需的面积代价，以及统计演化过程中的信息几何约束。

Method: 基于HBAR热力学对应（quantum-optical描述下近视界原子-场相互作用），在近稳态、热饱和区域推导面积成本定律（area-cost laws）；利用Fano不等式将解码错误概率转化为面积需求；并推导Fisher信息速度极限约束辐射场的统计演化与关联生成所需的最短持续时间。

Result: 建立了辐射场与其环境间可获取经典信息量和互信息的上界与视界面积预算的关系；通过Fano不等式将可靠性解码错误率映射为面积要求；得到Fisher信息速度极限，给出关联生成持续时间的下界；最终确立了bits-per-area原理。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了黑洞热力学、信息几何和量子信息三个领域，在HBAR框架下系统性地建立了面积-比特量化关系，为理解引力系统中的信息处理提供了几何化的理论工具。

Abstract: We develop a geometric theory of information processing in the Horizon-brightened acceleration radiation (HBAR) channel, in which the radiative horizon-area change provides an entropy budget for the information carried by the radiation field. Building on the quantum-optical description of atom--field interactions near the horizon and the resulting HBAR thermodynamic correspondence, we derive area-cost laws in the near-steady, thermally saturated regime. The accessible classical information and the mutual information generated between the radiation field and its environment are bounded by the associated radiative horizon-area budget. Reliability is incorporated through Fano's inequality, which translates a prescribed decoding error probability into an area requirement. We further derive Fisher-information speed limits that constrain the statistical evolution of the radiation field and place a lower bound on the duration required for correlation generation. Together, these results establish a bits-per-area principle linking black-hole thermodynamics, information geometry, and quantum information in the HBAR framework.

</details>


### [122] [Noise-aware emulation and cross-device validation of neutral atom analog quantum processing units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28364)
*Constantin Dalyac,Sergi Julià-Farré,Lucas Leclerc,Vittorio Vitale,Boris Albrecht,Lucas Béguin,Kemal Bidzhiev,Petru Borta,Clémence Briosne-Frejaville,Daniel J. Campbell,Dorian Claveau,Makrem Chatti,Antoine Cornillot,Julius de Hond,Anita Devi,Thomas Eritzpokhoff,Gaétan Hercé,Fergus Hayes,Soufiane Kaghad,Arun Kumar Abhimanyu,Lucas Lassablière,Mauro Mendizabal,Anton Quelle,Julien Ripoll,Henrique Silvério,Joseph Vovrosh,Guillaume Villaret,Adrien Signoles,Alexandre Dauphin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种噪声感知的仿真框架，用于预测基于里德伯原子阵列的模拟量子处理器的行为。该框架将主导噪声机制在整个计算周期中传播，并在三个Pasqal量子处理器上通过量子退火和淬火后动力学两种协议进行验证，所有测量观测值均落在仿真器预测的不确定性包络内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 里德伯原子阵列模拟量子处理器在量子多体模拟、组合优化和图机器学习等领域具有强大潜力，但随着设备日益普及，需要能定量连接微观硬件缺陷与实验结果的预测模型来建立对设备输出的信心。

Method: 构建一个噪声感知的仿真框架，在完整计算周期中传播主导噪声机制以预测设备行为；在三个Pasqal量子处理器上，以经典仿真仍可提供真值（ground truth）的两个代表性协议——量子退火和淬火后动力学——作为基准进行验证。

Result: 在全部三个设备上，实测观测量均落在仿真器预测的不确定性包络范围内；框架还能识别各运行区域中占主导地位的物理机制，并为算法设计和硬件改进提供定量指导。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅准确复现实验数据，还能为经典计算无法触及的模拟量子处理器构建验证基础，为模拟量子设备的可信输出建立了重要基础设施。

Abstract: Analog quantum processors based on Rydberg atom arrays are a powerful platform for many-body quantum simulation, combinatorial optimization, and graph machine learning. As these devices become increasingly accessible, establishing confidence in their outputs requires predictive models that quantitatively connect microscopic hardware imperfections to empirical results. Here, we present a noise-aware emulation framework that propagates the dominant noise mechanisms throughout the full computation cycle to predict device behavior. We validate the framework by benchmarking two representative protocols, quantum annealing and post-quench dynamics, on three Pasqal quantum processors where classical simulations still provide ground truth. Across all three devices, the measured observables fall within the uncertainty envelopes predicted by the emulator. Beyond reproducing the data, the framework isolates which physical mechanism dominates in each operating regime, provides quantitative guidance for algorithm design and hardware improvements, and establishes a foundation for verifying analog processors in regimes beyond classical reach.

</details>


### [123] [Bosonic quantum communication beyond the thermal threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27449)
*Francesco Anna Mele,Giuseppe Catalano,Marco Fanizza,Vittorio Giovannetti,Ludovico Lami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了玻色热衰减信道的量子容量。作者证明了Holevo-Werner在1999年提出的单次使用下界是所有单模高斯态的严格上确界，但发现非高斯态可以超越该下界。在非抗退化信道参数区域（如一个热光子、透射率η=0.8），高斯态的相干信息非正，但显式构造的秩二非高斯态（仅支撑在6个Fock能级上）可实现至少4.7×10⁻⁴ qubits/次信道的正相干信息；数值优化则达到8.4×10⁻³。在ν=1时，非高斯输入可在η≥0.7841时保证正的相干信息，而信道仅在η≤0.75时抗退化（量子容量为零），从而揭示了新的高噪声下可行的玻色量子通信区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色热衰减信道的量子容量归约于其相干信息的正则化，但该值长期未知。Holevo和Werner在1999年建立的单次使用下界（基于热态输入）一直未被证明是最优的。作者希望明确该下界是否严格，并探索是否可能存在超越高斯态输入的非高斯策略，以在高噪声区域确立正的量子容量。

Method: 作者首先严格证明Holevo-Werner下界等于对所有单模高斯态优化的相干信息的上确界；随后构造一个显式的秩二非高斯态（仅支撑在6个Fock能级上），通过数值验证（可能包括凸松弛或SDP验证）证明其在给定参数下产生正相干信息。他们还对固定的非高斯态族进行数值优化以获得更高的相干信息值。

Result: 证明了：(1) Holevo-Werner下界是所有单模高斯态相干信息的确切上确界；(2) 非高斯态可以超越该下界，即在η=0.8、一个热光子环境下，高斯输入相干信息非正，但秩二非高斯态的相干信息被认证至少为4.7×10⁻⁴ qubits/次信道，数值上非高斯族优化可达8.4×10⁻³；(3) 在ν=1时，非高斯输入在η≥0.7841即可保证正量子容量，而信道仅在η≤0.75时抗退化。

Conclusion: 这项工作首次证明高斯态在玻色热衰减信道相干信息优化中并非充分（非高斯态严格更优），并将量子通信的正容量区域从η>0.75（抗退化边界）扩展至η≥0.7841，揭示了新的高噪声玻色量子通信可行区域，深化了对玻色信道量子容量的理解。

Abstract: The quantum capacity of the bosonic thermal attenuator, which is given by the regularization of its coherent information, is unknown. The seminal work of Holevo and Werner established in 1999 the standard one-use lower bound obtained from input thermal states. We first prove that this long-standing lower bound is the exact supremum over all single-mode Gaussian states and then show that, crucially, a non-Gaussian state can do better. As a consequence, we prove positivity of the quantum capacity in a parameter region where the channel is not antidegradable, yet its coherent information optimized over single-mode Gaussian states vanishes. For example, with one thermal photon in the environment and at transmissivity $η=0.8$, the coherent information is non-positive for every single-mode Gaussian input. We give an explicit rank-two non-Gaussian state, supported on only six Fock levels, whose coherent information is certified to be at least $4.7\times10^{-4}$ qubits per channel use. This short witness is far from numerically optimal: a numerical optimization over fixed non-Gaussian families reaches at least $8.4\times 10^{-3}$ qubits per channel use at the same point. More generally, at $ν=1$, using non-Gaussian inputs we certify positivity of the coherent information, and therefore of the quantum capacity, down to $η=0.7841$; by contrast, the channel is antidegradable, and hence has zero quantum capacity, for $η\leq0.75$. Overall, our work identifies new high-noise regimes in which bosonic quantum communication is possible.

</details>


### [124] [Mitigating quantum decoherence via global optimal control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28479)
*Ashkan Abedi,Roberto Menta,Julien Despres,Marco Polini,Francesco Caravelli,Vittorio Giovannetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以准二维梯子几何构型的超导量子计算架构为原型，证明全局最优控制可以大幅抑制退相干的影响。通过张量网络方法量化了振幅衰减和退相位通道对量子信息流及单/双量子门保真度的损害，并展示了通过整形全局驱动可将门序列压缩约一个数量级的时间，恢复高保真度。这种缓解是非平凡的：在全局驱动的处理器中，耗散作用于每个物理量子比特（包括逻辑寄存器之外维持有序相的量子比特），无法通过保护孤立子系统来抑制，只能通过全局驱动的时间整形来克服。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局驱动的超导量子计算架构中，退相干对所有物理量子比特（包括逻辑寄存器之外的量子比特）都会造成损害，无法通过隔离子系统来保护，因此需要探索全局最优控制来抑制退相干的影响。

Method: 以准二维梯子几何构型为原型，采用张量网络方法量化振幅衰减和退相位通道对量子信息传播和门操作保真度的影响，并通过塑造全局驱动的时间轮廓来压缩门序列。

Result: 全局最优控制可将门序列在时间上压缩约一个数量级，从而恢复高门保真度，显著抑制退相干对量子信息流和门操作的影响。

Conclusion: 在全局驱动的超导量子计算架构中，仅通过全局驱动的时间整形即可克服普遍退相干的影响，无需保护孤立子系统，为大规模全局驱动量子处理器提供了可行的退相干缓解策略。

Abstract: We show that global optimal control can drastically suppress the impact of decoherence in globally driven superconducting quantum computing architectures, taking as a prototype a recently proposed quasi-two-dimensional ladder geometry. Using a tensor-network-based approach, we quantify how amplitude-damping and dephasing channels degrade the flow of quantum information along the ladder and the fidelity of one- and two-qubit gate operations. We then demonstrate that shaping the global drive compresses the gate sequences by an order of magnitude in time, restoring high gate fidelities. We stress that this mitigation is far from trivial: in a globally driven processor, dissipation acts on every physical qubit---including those outside the logical register that sustain the surrounding ordered phases---so its impact cannot be suppressed by protecting an isolated subsystem, and is instead overcome purely through the temporal shaping of the global drive.

</details>


### [125] [Coherent excitation of a two-state system by a Lorentzian field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27466)
*Aida Shiroyan,Genko S. Vasilev,Nikolay V. Vitanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有洛伦兹时间包络和恒定载频的场对二态量子系统的相干激发问题。该微分方程可精确地用合流Heun函数表示。针对跃迁概率，作者基于两个相关复过渡点及其干涉发展了Dykhne-Davis-Pechukas（DDP）描述，并在弱耦合和强耦合极限下直接解析分析了DDP作用量，获得了振荡相位、振荡包络、远失谐线形和近共振行为的显式渐近表达式。特别地，强耦合渐近结果表明共振奇数π脉冲的线宽与峰值Rabi频率成反比，展现出洛伦兹脉冲特有的功率窄化特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二态量子系统与特定时域包络场（如洛伦兹包络）的相互作用是非绝热量子动力学中的基础问题。虽然已有许多脉冲形状的研究，但对洛伦兹时间包络脉冲的相干激发缺乏完整的解析理论。理解该问题不仅具有基础理论意义，还能为实验中常见的脉冲形状提供精确的量子控制预测，尤其是线宽与功率关系的物理洞察。

Method: 作者首先将含时Schrödinger方程转化为合流Heun函数形式的精确解。随后采用Dykhne-Davis-Pechukas (DDP) 近似框架，在复时间平面上识别两个相关的复过渡点，并考虑它们之间的量子干涉路径以计算跃迁概率。DDP作用量在弱耦合和强耦合两个极限下分别进行解析展开，从而获得振荡相位、振荡包络、线形和近共振行为的显式渐近表达式。

Result: 得到了洛伦兹脉冲激发下跃迁概率在弱耦合和强耦合极限下的完整解析描述。其中关键的物理发现是：在共振奇数π脉冲条件下，强耦合渐近分析表明谱线宽度与峰值Rabi频率成反比关系，即Δω∝1/Ω₀，这体现了洛伦兹脉冲特有的功率窄化（power-narrowing）效应。

Conclusion: 该工作为洛伦兹时间包络脉冲激发二态系统提供了完整的解析理论框架，揭示了强耦合极限下的功率窄化现象，对理解非线性光学和量子控制中脉冲形状对跃迁线形的影响具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study the coherent excitation of a two-state quantum system by a field with a Lorentzian temporal envelope and constant carrier frequency. The associated differential equation admits an exact local representation in terms of confluent Heun functions. For the transition probability we develop a Dykhne-Davis-Pechukas (DDP) description based on the two relevant complex transition points and their interference. The DDP action is analyzed directly in the weak- and strong-coupling limits, yielding explicit asymptotic expressions for the oscillation phase, oscillation envelope, far-detuned line shape, and near-resonant behavior. In particular, the strong-coupling asymptotics imply a linewidth proportional to the inverse peak Rabi frequency for resonant odd-$π$ pulses, thereby exhibiting the power-narrowing characteristic of the Lorentzian pulse.

</details>


### [126] [Lifting Lifted Product Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28621)
*Yuta Hirasaki,Jong Yeon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于群扩展和图表提升(lift)的乘积(LP)码系统构造方法，通过保持Tanner图局部结构的同时增大码字尺寸，利用链映射和上链映射关联码参数、逻辑算子和容错逻辑操作。应用包括获得更优参数的LP码、跨有限提升传递code-surgery工具、开发平行乘积手术，以及将提升作为代数定义qLDPC码热力学族系统的第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘积(LP)码是一类具有优良码参数的量子纠错码，但现有构造缺乏系统性方法在保持局部结构的同时扩展码族，且code-surgery等容错逻辑操作在不同码族间的迁移和热力学族定义缺乏统一框架。

Method: 基于群扩展和图表提升构造LP码族，通过链映射和上链映射关联族内码参数与操作；结合所选有限提升传递code-surgery工具，开发平行乘积手术用于提升簇循环码，并将提升作为热力学族定义的系统步骤。

Result: 获得了优于既有报道参数的LP码；code-surgery工具可在有限提升间通过链映射传递并在多种情况下降低空间开销；对若干基础码和所选提升，相干信息呈现有限尺寸交叉现象，但结果也表明需补充条件才能确定唯一的热力学族。

Conclusion: 该工作为LP码族提供了系统性构造框架，在码参数、容错操作和热力学性质方面均有改进，但热力学族的唯一性仍需额外条件约束。

Abstract: Lifted product (LP) codes form an important class of quantum error correcting codes with favorable code parameters. We introduce a systematic construction of LP code families based on group extensions and graph lifts. The construction increases the code size while preserving the local structure of the Tanner graph, and relates code parameters, logical operators, and fault-tolerant logical-operation gadgets within the families through chain and cochain maps.
  As a first application, we obtain LP codes with better code parameters than previously reported ones. We then demonstrate that code-surgery gadgets can be transferred across the selected finite lifts through chain maps and, in several cases, implemented with lower space overhead. We also develop parallel product surgery for lifted clustered cyclic codes. Finally, we propose lifting as a systematic first step toward defining thermodynamic families for algebraically defined qLDPC codes without an underlying Euclidean lattice. For several base codes and selected lifts, coherent information exhibits finite-size crossings, while our results also indicate that additional conditions are needed to determine a unique family.

</details>


### [127] [Closed Timelike Curve Decoding on Quantum Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27473)
*Sai Nandan Morapakula,Kazuki Ikeda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了将Hayden–Preskill/Yoshida–Kitaev恢复映射嵌入Deutsch闭合类时曲线（D-CTC）一致性循环中的有限维电路模型。通过寄存器路由构建显式Deutsch映射，证明了当活动分支成功恢复消息时，CTC寄存器上的诱导映射为替换信道，其唯一不动点为恢复消息。作者在量子硬件上实现了Lloyd型后选择解码电路，并提出了经典反馈迭代方案来估计实验映射，结合Qiskit模拟和IBM硬件数据量化了解码保真度、后选择开销、路由相关噪声及量子几何敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deutsch闭合类时曲线（D-CTCs）通过固定点条件描述时间悖论区域，但将其与量子信息恢复协议（如Hayden–Preskill和Yoshida–Kitaev）相结合的实际电路实现尚不明确。作者旨在构建一个显式的有限维电路模型，将恢复映射嵌入D-CTC一致性循环中，并在真实量子硬件上进行实验验证，以连接理论时间旅行模型与可实现的量子计算实验。

Method: 采用寄存器路由构造使Deutsch映射显式化：初始SWAP将入射CTC态移至空闲转储寄存器，加扰器和解码器作用于剩余活动寄存器，最终SWAP将恢复消息写回CTC寄存器。当活动分支成功恢复消息时，诱导映射退化为替换信道σ↦ρ_M，其唯一不动点为ρ_M。实现Lloyd型后选择解码电路，并提出经典反馈迭代算法以估计实验映射。

Result: 通过Qiskit模拟和IBM量子硬件上的单量子比特实例，定量评估了解码保真度（decoder fidelity）、后选择开销（post-selection overhead）、路由相关噪声（routing-dependent noise）以及量子几何敏感性（quantum-geometric susceptibility），验证了理论预测的替换信道行为。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在真实量子硬件上实现了嵌入D-CTC一致性循环的恢复协议，证实了理论框架的在实验上的可行性，并为研究时间悖论电路的量子实现提供了可操作的方法论，同时指出了实际硬件噪声对CTC固定点条件满足程度的影响。

Abstract: Deutsch closed timelike curves (D-CTCs) are described by a fixed-point condition for a chronology-violating register. We study a finite-dimensional circuit model that places a Hayden--Preskill/Yoshida--Kitaev recovery map inside such a consistency loop. A register-routing construction makes the Deutsch map explicit: an initial SWAP moves the incoming CTC state to an idle dump register, the scrambler and decoder act on the remaining active registers, and a final SWAP writes the recovered message back to the CTC register. When the active branch recovers the message, the induced map on the CTC register is the replacement channel \(σ\mapsto ρ_M\), with the unique fixed point \(ρ_M\). We implement the associated Lloyd-type post-selected decoder circuits on quantum hardware and formulate a classical-feedback iteration for the experimentally estimated map. Qiskit simulations and IBM-hardware data for single-qubit instances quantify decoder fidelity, post-selection overhead, routing-dependent noise, and quantum-geometric susceptibility.

</details>


### [128] [Quantum Interference as a Proposal Mechanism for Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27509)
*Donald J. Jacobs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QIPS利用种子条件量子电路生成局部干涉图案作为有限次采样的提案分布，用于QUBO/Ising优化。在18≤n_b≤29的六个基准族上与匹配的经典对照组保持竞争性能，且提案预算与n_b成正比，证明局部量子干涉是资源高效的量子优化提案机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子干涉是否可以作为计算量子优化中资源高效的提案生成机制，而非依赖大规模量子态制备或变分参数优化。

Method: 构建种子条件量子电路生成局部干涉图案，作为有限次（100 shots/电路）提案分布；采样候选n_b位字符串后经典评分并更新精英前沿。采用固定两层门控电路架构，对照实验保持相同的搜索循环、前沿更新规则和提案预算。

Result: 在n_b=18至29的六个基准族中，QIPS与匹配的经典控制方法保持竞争性进展，总提案数与n_b成正比。使用top-K覆盖率、命中率、多重性、希尔伯特空间覆盖率和二进秩等指标评估。

Conclusion: 局部量子干涉被确认为一种资源高效的提案机制，能够在量子优化中在不扩大Hilbert空间采样成本的前提下保持竞争性的优化性能。

Abstract: Quantum Interference Proposal Search (QIPS) uses seed-conditioned quantum circuits to generate localized interference patterns as finite-shot proposal distributions for QUBO/Ising optimization. Candidate $n_b$-bit strings are sampled from these distributions, scored classically and used to update an elite frontier of low-energy solutions. QIPS uses a fixed two-layer gate-based circuit architecture with 100 shots per circuit while the Hilbert-space dimension grows as $2^{n_b}$. Across six benchmark families with $18 \le n_b \le 29$, QIPS maintains competitive progress relative to a matched classical control that preserves the same search loop, frontier update rule and proposal budget, with total proposals proportional to $n_b$. Performance is assessed using top-$K$ coverage, hit rate, multiplicity, Hilbert-space coverage and dyadic-rank metrics. The results identify localized quantum interference as a resource-efficient proposal mechanism for computational quantum optimization.

</details>


### [129] [Floquet Abelian Multicycle Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27521)
*Alexey A. Kovalev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了Floquet阿贝尔多周期（AMC）码，通过商格表示将一维D维AMC复形提升到时空并旋转ZX网络中的电路时间方向，实现了由本征双量子比特XX和ZZ测量组成的周期调度。在AMC4实例中获得了[[108,6,5]]、[[144,6,8]]和[[324,6,10]]的Floquet存储器参数，并在EM3噪声模型下估计伪阈值为约1.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMC码虽然具有紧凑的量子低密度奇偶校验结构和单次纠错能力，但其原始的高权重稳定子测量在物理实现上代价高昂。作者希望在不直接测量原始weight-six稳定子的前提下，利用Floquet方案提供仅测量的实现方式，从而更紧凑地实现高维同调冗余。

Method: 作者从一般level-j、D维AMC复形出发，推导了有限阿贝尔群代数上的商格表示；将该格提升到时空维度，并在关联的ZX网络中旋转电路时间方向。当检查和数据spider具有偶数值并允许面向时间的局部端口匹配时，网络的分解简化为XX和ZZ两比特测量的周期调度。随后通过最小化所有不等价电路切分来计算嵌入距离，并构造广义自行车码和level-2 AMC4实例。

Result: 针对局部等价于四维环面码的AMC4实例，获得了参数[[108,6,5]]、[[144,6,8]]和[[324,6,10]]的Floquet存储器。采用局部Pauli-web探测模板和束搜索解码，在测量本征EM3噪声模型下估计伪阈值约为1.2%。

Conclusion: 这项工作提供了高维同调冗余的紧凑仅测量实现方案，无需直接测量原始六权重稳定子，为实现更高维度的Floquet量子纠错存储器开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Abelian multicycle (AMC) codes are compact quantum low-density parity-check codes whose multiblock chain-complex structure provides redundant low-weight stabilizers and supports single-shot error correction. We introduce Floquet AMC codes by deriving a quotient-lattice representation of a general level-$j$, $D$-dimensional AMC complex over a finite Abelian group algebra, lifting this lattice to spacetime, and rotating the circuit-time direction in the associated ZX network. When the check and data spiders have even valence and admit a time-oriented local port matching, the network decomposes into a periodic schedule of native two-qubit $XX$ and $ZZ$ measurements. We construct generalized-bicycle and level-$2$ AMC4 examples, determine their instantaneous stabilizer groups, and compute their embedded distances by minimizing over all inequivalent circuit cuts. For AMC4 instances locally equivalent to four-dimensional toric codes, we obtain Floquet memories with parameters $[[108,6,5]]$, $[[144,6,8]]$, and $[[324,6,10]]$. Local Pauli-web detector templates and beam-search decoding under the measurement-native EM3 noise model yield an estimated pseudothreshold of approximately $1.2\%$. These results provide compact measurement-only realizations of higher-dimensional homological redundancy without directly measuring the original weight-six stabilizers.

</details>


### [130] [Phase Retrieval in $\mathbb C^4$ Requires Exactly Eleven Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27719)
*Meng Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用微分拓扑中的特征类与上同调群，证明C^4中不存在10个向量具有相位恢复性质，结合Vinzant的11向量构造，确定了C^4中相位恢复所需的最小测量数为11，并彻底解决了纯态量子层析、秩一POVM及幺正基数量等关联问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定C^4中相位恢复所需的最小强度测量数是一个长期悬而未决的开放问题。此前最好的结果仅能将最小数限定在10或11之间，无法确定确切值。该问题同时与纯态量子层析中信息完备POVM的元素数量、以及正交基数量等基础问题密切相关，解决它能填补理论空白并推动量子信息领域的发展。

Method: 本文采用微分拓扑中的特征类（characteristic classes）与上同调群（cohomology groups）作为核心数学工具，通过证明不存在10个向量在C^4中具备相位恢复性质，从而排除下界上的可能性；再结合Vinzant此前构造的显式11向量例子作为上界，最终将上下界精确锁定。

Result: 证明了C^4中没有任何10个向量的集合具有相位恢复性质，即最小测量数为11。由此得出：C^4上秩一POVM对纯态信息完备恰好需要11个元素；三个正交基不足以唯一区分C^4中的所有纯态，而四个正交基足够，因此恰好需要四个基。

Conclusion: 本文利用拓扑方法彻底解决了C^4相位恢复最小测量数这一长期开放问题，确定该数为11。该结论完全解决了Carmeli等人遗留的问题，同时澄清了纯态量子层析中信息完备POVM所需的精确元素数量以及正交基的精确数量。

Abstract: Determining the minimal number of intensity measurements required for phase retrieval in $\mathbb{C}^4$ has been a long-standing open problem. Prior to this work, the best-known results implied that this minimum was either $10$ or $11$. In this paper, we leverage characteristic classes and cohomology groups from differential topology to prove that no family of $10$ vectors in $\mathbb{C}^4$ possesses the phase retrieval property. Combining our lower bound with Vinzant's explicit eleven-vector construction establishes that the exact minimum is $11$. Our result yields a significant consequence for pure state quantum tomography, namely, a rank-one POVM on $\mathbb{C}^4$ requires exactly $11$ elements to be informationally complete for pure states. This further implies that three orthonormal bases are insufficient to uniquely distinguish all pure states in $\mathbb{C}^4$. Because four orthonormal bases are already known to be sufficient, we conclude that exactly four bases are required, thereby completely resolving the problem left in [C. Carmeli, T. Heinosaari, J. Schultz, A. Toigo, Eur. Phys. J. D].

</details>


### [131] [Efficient learning of bosonic unitaries beyond the Gaussian class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27534)
*Xiaobin Zhao,Quntao Zhuang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模量子酉变换的学习复杂度，指出学习困难的根本障碍并非非高斯性本身，而是不可约多模非高斯关联的积累。作者建立了玻色子酉学习可处理性边界：一般m模酉运算在每模能量约束E下至少需要Ω(E^{2m})次信道调用，而两类广泛的非高斯酉族（t掺杂高斯酉和Gaussian-Entanglable酉）可在多项式资源内学习。文中还提出了多模量子Darmois-Skitovich定理、非高斯过程几乎必然激活定理，以及从未校准相干探针学习酉变换的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子过程的表征学习是多模量子信息处理的核心挑战，尤其是超越高斯类别的非高斯过程，由于维度高且纠缠结构复杂而难以学习。作者希望澄清学习困难的真正根源，区分非高斯性本身与多模非高斯关联积累的不同作用，从而建立可学习的边界并开发高效学习协议。

Method: 作者采用前向（forward-only）学习协议，利用相干态探针、高斯操作、局域外差探测和经典后处理来识别全局高斯混合，将复杂任务约化为单模或少数模的学习。同时建立了学习下界的理论分析，并推导出多模量子Darmois-Skitovich定理和几乎必然激活定理作为理论基础。

Result: 建立了可处理性边界：一般m模酉运算学习需Ω(E^{2m})次信道调用（指数级），而两类非高斯酉族（t掺杂高斯酉和Gaussian-Entanglable酉）可用多项式资源学习。两类酉族可以同时具有广泛非高斯性和强多模纠缠。此外证明了模式扩展的被动网络仅在高斯输入态下保持乘积结构，以及非高斯酉对几乎所有相干输入态产生非高斯输出。

Conclusion: 学习的复杂度源于非高斯性与纠缠的不可约混合，而非任一资源单独所致。这一结论为多模量子过程的可学习性提供了基础性分类，区分了本质困难与可通过协议设计的可处理情形。

Abstract: Multimode quantum processes are generally difficult to learn, due to the large dimensionality and complex entanglement structure beyond the Gaussian class. Here, we show that the fundamental obstruction is not non-Gaussianity itself, but the buildup of irreducible multimode non-Gaussian correlations. We establish a tractability frontier for bosonic unitary learning: a general $m$-mode unitary with input energy at most $E$ per mode requires at least $Ω(E^{2m})$ channel uses, whereas two broad non-Gaussian families---$t$-doped Gaussian unitaries and Gaussian-entanglable unitaries---can be learned with resources polynomial in $m$. The latter can exhibit both extensive non-Gaussianity and strong multimode entanglement. Our forward-only protocols use coherent-state probes, Gaussian operations, local heterodyne detection, and classical post-processing to identify the global Gaussian mixing and reduce the remaining task to single- or few-mode learning. The analysis also yields a multimode quantum Darmois--Skitovich theorem showing that mode-spreading passive networks preserve product structure only for Gaussian input states, an almost-sure activation theorem for non-Gaussian processes showing that non-Gaussian unitaries yield non-Gaussian outputs for almost all coherent input states, and a method for learning unitaries from uncalibrated coherent probes. Our results identify that complexity of learning arises from irreducible mixing of non-Gaussianity and entanglement, rather than either resource alone.

</details>


### [132] [Exploring the use of quantum computing for facilitating spatially and temporally resolved models of a biological cell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27571)
*Muralikrishnan Gopalakrishnan Meena,Dileep Kishore,Jerry M. Parks,Luke Bertels,Dilipkumar N. Asthagiri,Travis Humble,Thomas L. Beck,Mitchel J. Doktycz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了量子计算能否为全细胞模拟提供可行路径，分析了从原子分子到代谢调控网络再到全细胞空间建模三个层级上量子算法的潜力，并进行复杂度对比分析，指出了数据编码、系统条件数、测量约束及量子-HPC混合集成等实际瓶颈，勾勒出量子加速全细胞建模的路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全细胞模拟是计算生物学的重大挑战——即使最简单的细胞也包含数千种相互作用的蛋白质和代谢物，空间跨越5个数量级（nm至μm）、时间跨越近19个数量级（fs至小时），且许多物理化学性质尚未完全表征。经典架构下进行全原子分辨率的全细胞周期模拟在计算上不可行，因此需要探索量子计算作为替代路径的可能性。

Method: 本文通过Perspective视角，将全细胞模拟划分为三个层级（原子级分子建模、代谢与调控网络、全细胞空间建模）进行分析；对代表性生物学问题进行了经典算法与量子算法的复杂度对比分析；总结了利用近期探索型与容错型量子架构的算法进展；识别了数据编码开销、系统条件数、测量约束和混合量子-HPC集成等实际瓶颈。

Result: 在特定算法假设下，量子算法在代表性生物学问题上展现出显著的理论加速潜力；同时识别出数据编码开销、系统条件数、测量约束及量子-HPC混合集成四大类关键瓶颈，这些瓶颈决定了量子优势能否在实际全细胞模拟场景中兑现。

Conclusion: 文章勾勒了量子加速全细胞建模的路线图，认为量子计算在多尺度全细胞模拟框架中具有潜在价值，但需先在数据编码、系统求解条件和混合计算架构等方面取得突破，才能实现所承诺的生物学洞察。

Abstract: Whole-cell simulation, modeling all of a cell's functional systems over its life cycle, is an outstanding challenge in computational biology. Even the simplest living cell contains thousands of interacting proteins and metabolites (on the order of trillions of atoms) whose full functional dynamics spans roughly five orders of magnitude in space (nm to $μ$m) and nearly nineteen in time (fs to hours). Further, many of the governing physical and chemical properties remain incompletely characterized. Simulating such complex systems at fully atomistic resolution over a full cell cycle is computationally intractable on classical architectures, raising a central question: Can quantum computing offer a viable path to whole-cell simulations that integrate molecular- and systems-level complexity? This Perspective examines the potential of quantum computing across three hierarchical scales: atomistic-molecular modeling, metabolic and regulatory networks, and whole-cell spatial modeling. We present a complexity analysis comparing classical and quantum algorithms for representative biological problems, identifying regimes of substantial theoretical speedup under specified algorithmic assumptions. We highlight algorithmic developments designed to leverage both near-term exploratory and fault-tolerant quantum architectures, and discuss practical bottlenecks: data encoding overhead, system conditioning, measurement constraints, and hybrid quantum-HPC integration. Together, these results outline a roadmap for quantum-accelerated whole-cell modeling and the biological insights such multiscale frameworks may eventually enable.

</details>


### [133] [Quantum LDPC codes with design rate 1/5 and good performance below 1000 physical qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27644)
*Yifan Hong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的量子LDPC码族，设计码率为1/5、校验权重为9，在数百个物理量子比特下接近teraquop内存机制，并通过GPU加速的Relay-BP解码实现1-2ms的平均延迟，适用于捕获离子和中性原子处理器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恒定速率量子LDPC码虽然理论上承诺渐近极限下的恒定空间开销，但在实践中发现具有良好性能的有限长度码实例仍然非常困难，需要新的构造方法来解决这一挑战。

Method: 该方法采用经典LDPC码的平衡积构造，利用共享非阿贝尔Z_l ⋊ Z_m群对称性的码字；针对可重构原子阵列设计基于贪心调度器的综合征提取电路；并构造关于群对称等变的逻辑Pauli基以压缩代码手术的设计空间。

Result: 在0.1%无空闲电路级噪声和GPU加速Relay-BP解码（平均延迟约1-2ms）下，数百个物理量子比特即可接近teraquop内存机制；单轮重排时间约30-60ms（基于现有硬件规格），且具有显著的优化空间。

Conclusion: 这些结果进一步推进了恒定速率量子LDPC码在近期容错量子计算机中的实用性，为捕获离子和中性原子平台提供了切实可行的容错量子存储方案。

Abstract: Constant-rate quantum low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes promise fault-tolerant quantum computation with constant spatial overhead in the asymptotic limit. Nonetheless, discovering finite-length code instances with good practical performance remains challenging. We introduce a new family of quantum LDPC codes with design rate $1/5$ and check weight $9$ that approaches the teraquop memory regime per qubit-round with several hundred physical qubits, under idling-free circuit-level noise of strength $0.1\%$ and GPU-accelerated Relay-belief-propagation (Relay-BP) decoding with average latencies around 1-2 ms, a regime relevant to trapped-ion and neutral-atom processors. The construction involves the balanced product of classical LDPC codes with design rate $1/2$ that share non-abelian $\mathbb{Z}_\ell \rtimes \mathbb{Z}_m$ group symmetries, which may be of independent interest for classical error correction. We build syndrome extraction circuits tailored to reconfigurable atom arrays using a simple greedy scheduler, with single-round rearrangement times around 30-60 ms using present hardware specifications, and substantial room for future improvements. We also construct logical Pauli bases that are equivariant with respect to the group symmetry, which can significantly compress the design space for code surgery. Together, these results further advance the practicality of constant-rate quantum LDPC codes for near-term, fault-tolerant quantum computers.

</details>


### [134] [Quadruply Bonded Mo2 Molecules: An Innate Emitter-Resonator Quantum System in Free Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27683)
*Miao Meng,Ying Ning Tan,Zi Cong He,Zi Hao Zhong,Jia Zhou,Yu Li Zhou,Guang Yuan Zhu,Chun Y. Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明具有极短M-Mo键（2.1Å）的四重键Mo₂单元可作为固有发射器-谐振器分子量子系统，在环境条件下将可见光光子囚禁于两个钼原子之间，产生具有极小模式体积的强量化局域电磁场。三种Mo₂配合物的共振荧光光谱显示金属间电荷转移跃迁与局域散射场相干耦合，表现出真空拉比劈裂和Mollow三重态，确立了Mo₂分子作为独立的发射器-谐振器集成量子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子电动力学从传统原子/光子体系扩展到分子科学，探索金属-金属键合体系中原生的量子光学现象，构建无需复杂腔体即可在自由空间中实现量子光学实验的分子级集成量子系统。

Method: 利用四重键Mo₂单元的极短M-Mo键距（2.1Å）作为固有纳米腔；通过共振荧光光谱测量三种Mo₂配合物，分析金属间电荷转移跃迁与散射场的相干耦合；同时比较单分子与N分子系综的侧带激发行为，并与Ni₂基体系进行对比验证。

Result: 观察到真空拉比劈裂和Mollow三重态等强耦合量子光学特征；单分子及N分子系综的侧带激发产生跨越宽波长范围的离散光学模式序列；极化的光学跃迁与Ni₂基体系一致，证实了该现象的普适性。

Conclusion: Mo₂分子是一个独立的自包含发射器-谐振器集成量子系统，能够使用常规光谱仪器在自由空间中进行量子光学实验。该工作将腔量子电动力学拓展至分子科学，为金属-金属键合、分子物理及光-物质相互作用提供了新见解。

Abstract: In recent decades, significant progress has been made in constructing and studying individual quantum systems based on two level atoms (molecules) and photons. Here we demonstrate that the quadruply bonded Mo2 unit, with a MoMo bond distance as short as 2.1A, functions as an innate emitter resonator molecular quantum system capable of trapping visible light photons between the two molybdenum atoms under ambient conditions, thereby generating an intense quantized local electromagnetic field with an extremely small mode volume. The resonance fluorescence spectra of three Mo2 complexes indicate that the intermetallic Mo-Mo charge transfer transition is coherently coupled to the local scattering field, exhibiting vacuum Rabi splitting and Mollow triplets. Resonant coupling of single molecules and N-molecule ensembles to the scattered light through sideband excitation produces a sequence of discrete optical modes spanning a broad wavelength range, with polaritonic transitions identical to those observed in Ni2 based systems. These results establish the Mo2 molecule as an independent emitter resonator integrated quantum system that enables quantum optical experiments in free space using conventional spectroscopic instrumentation. This work extends quantum electrodynamics into molecular science, providing new insights into metal metal bonding, molecular physics, and light matter interactions.

</details>


### [135] [Quantum coherence and entanglement in wireless quantum batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27718)
*Jun-Jie Jiang,Jin-Di Cao,Yu-Fei Zhang,Meng-Long Song,Dong Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由共同结构化玻色环境介导的无线量子电池系统，通过资源理论分析阐明了非马尔可夫记忆效应与耦合对称性在能量传输调控中的不同作用，并揭示了两类量子相干（一阶相干与l1范数相干）在功提取中的差异化功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子电池在环境辅助下的充电动力学与热力学性能，理解非马尔可夫效应和耦合对称性如何调控能量传输，为环境辅助量子储能器件的工程化设计提供理论框架。

Method: 采用统一的资源理论分析方法，分别研究马尔可夫弱耦合与非马尔可夫强耦合两种机制下的充电动力学；通过l1范数相干性、一阶相干性、纠缠振荡与熵（ergotropy）等指标进行热力学与量子信息层面的综合表征。

Result: 发现了两个关键机制：(1) 马尔可夫弱耦合下，l1范数相干的动态重建可补偿一阶相干的单调衰减以维持能量输运；(2) 非马尔可夫强耦合下，环境回流诱导纠缠与储能同步振荡的协同共振。耦合对称性可作为控制参数：不对称耦合在无记忆环境中优化能量增益，对称耦合在强相互作用下激活暗态保护机制。热力学分析表明一阶相干设定非相干功的激活阈值，l1范数相干作为相干功提取的显式燃料。

Conclusion: 该研究为环境辅助的量子能量存储设备提供了精细化的理论设计框架，明确了非马尔可夫记忆效应、耦合对称性及不同相干类型在量子电池充电和功提取中的具体角色，有望指导更高效的量子电池工程实现。

Abstract: We investigate the charging dynamics and thermodynamic performance of a wireless quantum battery system mediated by a common structured bosonic environment. By employing a unified resource-theoretic analysis, we elucidate the distinct roles of non-Markovian memory effects and coupling symmetry in regulating energy transfer. In the Markovian weak-coupling regime, we identify a transformative mechanism where the dynamic reconstruction of $l_1$-norm coherence compensates for the monotonic decay of first-order coherence to sustain energy transport. Conversely, the non-Markovian strong-coupling regime facilitates a cooperative resonance, characterized by the synchronized oscillation of entanglement and stored energy induced by environmental backflow. Furthermore, we reveal that coupling symmetry acts as a critical control parameter: while asymmetric coupling favoring the battery optimizes energy gain in memoryless environments, symmetric coupling under strong interactions unlocks a dark-state protection mechanism, effectively trapping energy within a decoherence-free subspace. Finally, a thermodynamic analysis based on ergotropy demonstrates that first-order coherence establishes the activation threshold for incoherent work, whereas $l_1$-norm coherence serves as the explicit fuel for coherent work extraction. These findings provide a refined theoretical framework for engineering environment-assisted quantum energy storage devices.

</details>


### [136] [Exact Incompatibility-Breaking Criterion for Unital Qubit Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27757)
*Yujie Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究噪声量子比特信道使得所有POVM(正算子值测度)联合可测量的条件。对于每个酉信道(unital qubit channel)，推导出精确的"破坏不相容性"判据，并证明投影测量已足以确定任意POVM的边界。通过steering-联合可测量性对应关系，得到所有具有最大混合边缘态(maximally mixed marginals)的两比特态的精确POVM-steering边界，Werner态为特例。对于非酉信道，构造了一般非对称父POVM，获得显式的充分不相容性破坏条件，进而得到任意两比特态的充分不可导引性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究噪声量子信道何时能使所有POVM变得联合可测量(即信道破坏测量不相容性)，以及通过steering-联合可测量性对应，为量子导引(steering)提供精确边界判据。

Method: 对酉信道推导精确不相容性破坏判据；利用投影测量确定任意POVM的边界；借助steering-联合可测量性对应关系建立两比特态导引判据；对非酉信道构造非对称父POVM以获得充分条件。

Result: 给出了所有酉量子比特信道的精确不相容性破坏判据；得到了具有最大混合边缘态的两比特态的精确POVM-steering边界(Werner态为特例)；对非酉信道给出了显式充分条件及一般两比特态的充分不可导引性判据。

Conclusion: 完整解决了酉量子比特信道的POVM不相容性破坏问题，并将结果推广到非酉情形(充分条件层面)，为量子导引的判定提供了统一的框架。

Abstract: We study when a noisy qubit channel renders all positive-operator-valued measures (POVMs) jointly measurable. For every unital qubit channel, we derive the exact incompatibility-breaking criterion and prove that projective measurements already determine the boundary for arbitrary POVMs. Through the steering--joint-measurability correspondence, this result gives the exact POVM-steering boundary for all two-qubit states with maximally mixed marginals, with the Werner states recovered as a special case. For nonunital qubit channels, we construct a generally asymmetric parent POVM and obtain an explicit sufficient incompatibility-breaking condition, which in turn yields a sufficient unsteerability criterion for arbitrary two-qubit states.

</details>


### [137] [Resource-efficient quantum-selected configuration interaction for molecular properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27777)
*Suprava Sahoo,Abdul Kalam,Kenji Sugisaki,V. S. Prasannaa,B. P. Das*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过识别优势费米子激发算符和参考态保真度损失分析来构建紧凑哈密顿量的方法，以减少量子选择组态相互作用（QSCI）在噪声量子设备上的计算开销。将该框架应用于IIIA族单氟化物（BF、AlF、GaF、InF、TlF），实现了哈密顿量项数接近二次方的缩减，并在IBM Marrakesh处理器上成功运行了多达20量子比特的AlF和TlF系统，电路深度和双量子比特门数减少超过98%，偶极矩与精确值吻合度达99.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子选择组态相互作用（QSCI）需要实现完整的电子哈密顿量，但在噪声量子设备上，这会导致电路复杂度迅速增加，严重限制了方法的可扩展性。因此需要一种策略来降低哈密顿量的计算开销，同时保持高精度。

Method: 通过识别主要的费米子激发算符并进行参考态保真度损失分析，构建紧凑哈密顿量（保留关键项、舍弃次要项），从而减少QSCI框架所需的量子资源开销。将该框架应用于IIIA族单氟化物系统进行相对论基态能量和永久电偶极矩的计算，并通过量子硬件实验验证。

Result: 该框架在哈密顿量项数上实现了接近二次方的缩减。在IBM Marrakesh处理器上，20量子比特的TlF系统电路深度和双量子比特门数减少超过98%，量子硬件计算得到的永久电偶极矩与完整活性空间组态相互作用（CASCI）值的吻合度超过99.99%。

Conclusion: 该紧凑哈密顿量构建方法显著降低了QSCI在噪声中等规模量子（NISQ）设备上的资源需求，展示了其在NISQ硬件上实现大规模高精度量子化学模拟的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: The quantum-selected configuration interaction identifies important determinantal basis functions through real-time evolution of a reference wavefunction and diagonalizing the Hamiltonian matrix in the resulting selected subspace. However, implementing the full electronic Hamiltonian on noisy quantum devices leads to rapidly increasing circuit complexity, limiting its scalability. To address this issue, we identify the dominant fermionic excitation operators and perform reference-state fidelity loss analysis to construct a compact Hamiltonian, reducing computational overhead while retaining high precision. Applied to Group IIIA monofluorides (BF, AlF, GaF, InF, and TlF), the proposed framework achieves a near-quadratic improvement in Hamiltonian-term scaling, enabling resource-efficient simulations. We employ this framework to compute the relativistic ground-state energies and permanent electric dipole moments (PDMs) of the systems under consideration. After validating the framework via simulations, we demonstrate hardware execution for AlF and TlF on the IBM Marrakesh processor using active spaces of up to 20 qubits. For a 20-qubit TlF system, the reduced Hamiltonian yields a reduction of higher than $ 98\%$ in both circuit depth and two-qubit gate counts, with the resulting PDMs from quantum hardware matching complete active space configuration interaction values within $99.99\%$. These results demonstrate the scalability of this approach on noisy intermediate-scale quantum devices.

</details>


### [138] [Quantum machine learning interatomic potential: Application of variational quantum algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27841)
*Kohei Numata,Wataru Mizukami,Kosuke Mitarai,Keisuke Fujii,Yutaka Imamura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了将量子电路学习（混合量子-经典机器学习算法）应用于机器学习原子间势（MLIP），用于预测分子数据集中分子的能量。作者采用量子迁移学习架构重训练了ANI模型，并通过量子电路模拟器评估数值精度。结果表明，在特定条件下，在经典神经网络中插入量子电路比全经典神经网络精度略高，尤其是当预训练模型精度提升空间较大时，量子电路模型更为有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子机器学习在机器学习原子间势（MLIP）领域的应用潜力，检验量子电路学习能否提升分子能量预测的准确性，为量子机器学习在该方向的发展提供实证依据。

Method: 采用量子电路学习这一混合量子-经典机器学习算法，基于量子迁移学习架构（Mari等人2020年提出）重训练ANI模型，使用量子电路模拟器进行数值精度评估，并与全经典神经网络进行对比。

Result: 将量子电路插入MLIP的经典神经网络中，在特定条件下比全经典神经网络的精度略高；当预训练模型存在较大精度提升空间时，嵌入量子电路的模型表现更为有效。

Conclusion: 量子机器学习在MLIP应用中展现出可行性，量子电路嵌入经典网络可带来精度上的增益，尤其在基座模型精度受限的场景下，这为量子机器学习在原子间势领域的进一步应用提供了支持。

Abstract: This study applied quantum circuit learning, a commonly used hybrid quantum-classical machine learning algorithm, to a machine learning interatomic potential (MLIP) for predicting the energies of molecules in molecular datasets. We retrained the ANI model using the quantum transfer learning architecture [Mari et al., Quantum, 4:340, 2020] and evaluated numerical accuracy with a quantum circuit simulator. The evaluation confirmed that inserting a quantum circuit into the classical neural network of the MLIP yielded slightly higher accuracy than the fully classical neural network under certain conditions. In particular, the model incorporating a quantum circuit was more effective when the pretraining model had room for improvement in accuracy. These findings may contribute to advancing the application of quantum machine learning for MLIPs.

</details>


### [139] [Even-harmonic generation through nonequilibrium steady-state symmetry breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27847)
*Milad Jangjan,Arman Kashef,Siamak Pooyan,Stefan Scheel,Dieter Bauer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了在保持哈密顿量反转对称性不变的情况下，通过边界驱动的SSH链与储库耦合产生的耗散动力学，在非平衡稳态中形成有限直流电流，从而打破系统密度矩阵的反演对称性，使原本被禁止的偶次谐波在高次谐波谱中出现，并在输运电流与HHG选择定则之间建立了直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心对称系统中高次谐波产生通常因反演对称性而被限制为奇次谐波，传统观点认为打破该选择定则需要对哈密顿量本身进行改性。作者希望探究是否存在不修改底层哈密顿量、仅依靠非平衡稳态物理来打破该对称性的机制。

Method: 采用基于Lindblad主方程的关联矩阵方法，研究边界驱动的Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）链耦合到源和汇储库的系统，计算非平衡稳态密度矩阵及其对应的HHG响应，分析偶次谐波强度与输运电流之间的关系。

Result: 发现耗散动力学产生了携带有限直流电流的非平衡稳态；尽管SSH哈密顿量保持反演对称性，但载流稳态密度矩阵打破了该对称性，从而在发射谱中出现了偶次谐波。偶次谐波强度直接受输运电流控制。

Conclusion: 揭示了纯粹依赖非平衡稳态对称性破缺产生偶次谐波的机制，为通过超快非线性光谱探测中心对称量子系统中的输运电流提供了新途径。

Abstract: High-harmonic generation (HHG) in inversion-symmetric systems is typically restricted to odd harmonics by symmetry. Here, we show that this selection rule can be broken without modifying the underlying Hamiltonian. We investigate a boundary-driven Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chain coupled to source and sink reservoirs and demonstrate that dissipative dynamics generates a nonequilibrium steady state carrying a finite DC current. While the SSH Hamiltonian retains inversion symmetry, the current-carrying steady-state density matrix does not, leading to the emergence of even harmonics in the emitted spectrum. Using a correlation-matrix approach based on the Lindblad master equation, we obtain the steady state and calculate the resulting HHG response. We find that the intensity of the even harmonics is directly controlled by the transport current, establishing a link between nonequilibrium charge transport and HHG selection rules. Our results uncover a mechanism for even-harmonic generation that relies solely on nonequilibrium steady-state symmetry breaking and provide a route to probing transport currents through ultrafast nonlinear spectroscopy in centrosymmetric quantum systems.

</details>


### [140] [Beating the Bad-Cavity Limit via Auxiliary-Emitter Linewidth Squeezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27878)
*Youke Xu,Zeyang Liao,Xue-hua Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过引入两个具有相反失谐的非相同辅助发射体到弱腔中，利用亚辐射模式压缩有效腔线宽，形成超窄透射窗口，使目标发射体在裸腔处于弱耦合条件下仍能表现出强耦合特征（如持久的真空Rabi振荡和自发辐射劈裂）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腔QED中的强耦合依赖于高腔品质因子或超小模体积，这两者之间存在根本性权衡，限制了实际应用。作者希望打破这一约束，在"差腔"（bad cavity）条件下实现有效的强耦合物理。

Method: 在品质因子较低的腔中放入两个失谐符号相反（非相同）的辅助发射体，构成混合系统。该系统支持一个亚辐射模式，能够显著压缩有效腔线宽，在腔频率处产生超窄透射窗口，从而为置于其中的目标发射体提供工程化的强耦合环境。

Result: 目标发射体在该工程化环境中表现出持久的真空Rabi振荡和分辨的自发辐射劈裂，这些是强耦合的明确特征，尽管裸腔本身仍处于弱耦合区间。

Conclusion: 该方案将差腔转换为有效的强耦合物理平台，为量子计算和量子传感提供了潜在应用。

Abstract: Strong coupling in cavity QED is conventionally achieved at the expense of either high cavity quality factors or ultrasmall mode volumes, a trade-off that fundamentally constrains practical implementations. Here, we circumvent this limitation by introducing two nonidentical auxiliary emitters with opposite detunings into a bad cavity. This hybrid system supports a subradiant mode that significantly squeeze the effective cavity linewidth, creating an ultra-narrow transmission window at the cavity frequency. As a result, a target emitter placed in this engineered environment exhibits prolonged vacuum Rabi oscillations and resolved spontaneous emission splitting, which are clear signatures of strong coupling, even though the bare cavity remains in the weak-coupling regime. Our scheme thus transforms a bad cavity into an effective platform for strong-coupling physics, with potential applications in quantum computation and quantum sensing.

</details>


### [141] [Trainability and Mode Separation of Mixed IQP-QCBMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27883)
*Youngseok Lee,Hyunwoo Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出混合IQP-QCBM模型，将具有辅助比特的IQP电路推广为多个无辅助IQP电路（分支）的加权混合，在多项式分支数下证明其可避免局部贫瘠高原，并提出簇初始化以实现模式分离，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于IQP电路的量子电路Born机（QCBM）具有经典可训练性优势，但其无辅助比特形式虽避免贫瘠高原却不具备普适性。添加辅助比特虽提升表达能力，但其是否保持局部可训练性未知。因此需要一种既能保持可训练性又能提升表达能力的模型。

Method: 提出混合IQP-QCBM，将辅助比特扩展电路泛化为多个无辅助IQP电路（称为分支）的加权混合。在多项式分支数下，证明从数据无关初始化以及（在特定假设下）数据相关初始化出发可避免局部贫瘠高原。进一步提出簇初始化，为每个分支分配不同的无监督数据簇以提供初始模式分离。在16位数据集上进行精确计算验证，并在多个基准数据集（二值簇、二维Ising模型、二值化MNIST、484自旋玻璃）上实验。

Result: 证明了混合IQP-QCBM在多项式分支数下可避免局部贫瘠高原；只有分支生成不同分布时才能超越最好的无辅助IQP电路。发现模式分离难以从生成相同分布的分支初始化获得，因为分离梯度被抑制。簇初始化在四个基准上收敛最快且达到最低的平均测试MMD^2，且达到最优时各分支专门化到可区分的数据特征（如团块模式、磁化扇区、数字形状）。

Conclusion: 混合IQP-QCBM通过分支加权混合兼顾了可训练性与表达性，簇初始化通过赋予各分支不同的数据簇实现了模式分离，从而在生成质量上显著优于无辅助IQP-QCBM及其他初始化策略。

Abstract: Quantum circuit Born machines (QCBMs) based on instantaneous quantum polynomial-time (IQP) circuits are promising quantum generative models for their classical trainability. It is known that their ancilla-free form avoids barren plateaus under certain initializations, but remains non-universal. Although adding ancilla qubits raises the expressivity, whether the ancilla-extended model retains local trainability remains unknown. We propose the mixed IQP-QCBM, which generalizes the ancilla-extended circuit as a weighted mixture of ancilla-free IQP circuits, called branches. For a polynomial number of branches, we prove local barren-plateau avoidance from data-agnostic and, under certain assumptions, data-dependent initializations. We further show that the mixed IQP-QCBM can surpass the best ancilla-free IQP circuit only if its branches generate a number of distinct distributions. In particular, we focus on a behavior we call \emph{mode separation}, in which each branch captures a particular feature of the target. Mode separation is hard to attain from an initialization whose branches generate the same distribution: the gradients that would separate them are suppressed while the distributions they generate remain close. This motivates \emph{cluster initialization}, which assigns a different unsupervised data cluster to each branch and provides an initial degree of mode separation. Exact calculations on two 16-bit datasets support the barren-plateau and gradient-suppression claims. On four benchmarks, binary clusters, a two-dimensional Ising model, binarized MNIST, and a 484-spin glass, cluster initialization converges fastest and reaches the lowest mean test $\mathrm{MMD}^2$. We observe that, when achieving the lowest test $\mathrm{MMD}^2$, the mixed IQP-QCBM contains branches specialized to distinguishable data features such as blob patterns, magnetization sectors, or digit shapes.

</details>


### [142] [Witness robustness: An operational quantifier of measurement resources via free state discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27892)
*Jingsong Ao,Aby Philip,Alexander Streltsov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子测量的"见证鲁棒性"（witness robustness）这一资源量化指标，其允许的噪声是自由态见证元（free-state witnesses）的元组而非物理测量。该指标具有操作解释：量化一个测量相对于自由测量在鉴别完全由自由态构成的系综时的最大优势。与标准和广义鲁棒性不同，见证鲁棒性一般不具忠实性，反映了一个资源性测量在只能制备自由态时未必有用这一事实。论文识别了恢复忠实性的条件，并证明在具有资源摧毁映射的资源理论中，所有测量的见证鲁棒性为零。还建立了凸性和单调性等基本性质，并针对单量子比特魔法理论中的投影测量和两量子比特PPT纠缠理论中的二值纯态投影测量导出了解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准鲁棒性和广义鲁棒性作为量子测量资源量化工具，其噪声来自物理测量，且总是忠实的。然而，在只能制备自由态的框架下，资源性测量未必能提供实际优势，因此需要一种新的鲁棒性概念，其噪声允许自由态见证元，以更精确地刻画测量在自由态系综鉴别中的实际价值。

Method: 论文定义了见证鲁棒性，将噪声空间从物理测量扩展为自由态见证元的元组，并建立其操作解释。通过分析忠实性条件，识别资源摧毁映射与见证鲁棒性为零之间的关联；通过证明凸性和单调性确立其作为资源量化指标的合法性；最后在单量子比特魔法理论和两量子比特PPT纠缠理论中推导投影测量的解析表达式。

Result: 证明了见证鲁棒性具有清晰的操作意义（最大鉴别优势），但不具备普遍忠实性；在存在资源摧毁映射的理论中，所有测量的见证鲁棒性恒为零；确立了凸性和单调性；在单量子比特魔法理论及两量子比特PPT纠缠理论的投影测量上给出了解析结果。

Conclusion: 见证鲁棒性提供了一种新的量子测量资源量化框架，它揭示了"资源丰富"与"在自由态系综中实际有用"之间的本质区别，为量子资源理论中测量的操作价值提供了更精细的工具。

Abstract: We introduce the witness robustness of quantum measurements, a resource quantifier whose admissible noise consists of tuples of free-state witnesses rather than physical measurements. We establish its operational interpretation: it quantifies the maximal advantage that a measurement can provide over free measurements in discriminating an ensemble composed entirely of free states. Unlike the standard and generalized robustnesses, the witness robustness is not faithful in general, reflecting the fact that a resourceful measurement need not be useful when only free states can be prepared. We identify conditions under which faithfulness is recovered and show that, in resource theories admitting a resource-destroying map, the witness robustness vanishes for every measurement. We also establish fundamental properties, including convexity and monotonicity. Finally, we derive analytical results for projective measurements in single-qubit magic and for binary pure-state projective measurements in the two-qubit PPT entanglement theory.

</details>


### [143] [Interconnection of Quantum Networks at Urban scale: Analysis of Temporal Stability of Entangled Photon Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27906)
*Laura d'Avossa,Angela Sara Cacciapuoti,Marcello Caleffi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了纠缠量子网络中基于纠缠光子源的时钟同步稳定性，在电信C波段进行实验，证明光源锁定后8小时内时间相关性稳定（最大漂移约120 ps），并在真实城域光纤网络上实现两个纠缠光子源的实验同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠量子网络中，时间同步是基本要求，光子不可区分性对Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉和纠缠交换至关重要。除精确的时间对准外，参考时钟的长期稳定性同样关键，微小波动会降低网络整体性能。因此需要在真实环境下验证时钟同步的稳定性。

Method: 基于电信C波段的纠缠光子源，采用时间标记符合测量分析光子探测事件之间的时间相关性，提取同步峰及其长期稳定性。在受控实验室条件和真实城域电信基础设施（具有损耗和背景噪声）两种场景下进行实验。

Result: 锁定后光源在8小时采集期间表现出稳定的时间相关性，最大漂移约120 ps，主要与长光纤链路相关。相关峰宽度与探测器抖动一致，无额外系统引入的时间展宽。在城域部署中，尽管存在显著非理想因素，观察到的相关性仍清晰可检测，且与高斯统计近似良好吻合。

Conclusion: 两个纠缠光子源不仅在受控实验室条件下可以可靠同步，在真实世界运行约束下也能实现可靠同步，验证了该同步方案在实际量子网络部署中的可行性。

Abstract: Time synchronization is a fundamental requirement in entanglement-based quantum networks, where the indistinguishability of photons in the time domain is essential for enabling Hong-Ou-Mandel interference and entanglement swapping. In addition to precise temporal alignment, it is equally crucial to ensure the stability of the reference clock over time, as even small fluctuations can degrade the overall performance of the network. In this work, we investigate the stability of clock synchronization for entanglement distribution based on entangled-photon sources operating in the telecommunication C-band. Temporal correlations between photon detection events are analyzed using time-tagged coincidence measurements, enabling the extraction of synchronization peaks and their long-term stability. Experimental results demonstrate that, once locked, the sources exhibit stable temporal correlations over an 8-hour acquisition period, with a maximum observed drift of approximately 120 ps, primarily associated with long fiber links. The width of the correlation peak remains consistent with detector jitter, indicating negligible additional system-induced temporal broadening. A central result of this work is the experimental synchronization between two entanglement-photon sources in a realistic metropolitan deployment, where entanglement distribution is performed over existing telecommunication infrastructure characterized by non-negligible losses and background noise. In this scenario, despite the presence of significant imperfections introduced by the metropolitan-scale fiber network, the observed correlations remain clearly detectable and are consistently well-approximated by Gaussian statistics. This confirms that the two sources can be reliably synchronized not only in controlled laboratory conditions but also under real-world operating constraints.

</details>


### [144] [Iterative quantum algorithms for the minimum vertex cover problem based on continuous-time quantum walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27915)
*Ruben Pariente Bassa,Finley A. Quinton,Franz G. Fuchs,Pascal Halffmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种约束保持的混合量子-经典贪心框架，用于求解最小顶点覆盖问题，并可通过对偶补集扩展到最大独立集问题。框架利用投影Pauli-X项保持可行子空间，实现连续时间量子行走在可行覆盖分层图上的演化，并用量子信息指导贪心递归归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小顶点覆盖和最大独立集是经典的NP难组合优化问题，在近似算法中具有核心地位。传统量子方法通常需要惩罚项或变分训练，存在电路深度大、收敛不可靠等问题。作者希望设计一种无需惩罚项、无变分训练的约束保持量子框架，利用量子行走的相干传播特性来指导贪心策略，从而超越经典贪心基线。

Method: 框架核心包括：(1) 构造投影Pauli-X项，其求和算子仅在可行子空间内有效，并精确等价于可行覆盖分层图的邻接矩阵；(2) 该图连通，保证从全含顶点集出发可通过单顶点翻转序列到达任意可行覆盖；(3) 以全含顶点配置为初态执行连续时间量子行走，使振幅传播到包含更小覆盖的分层中；(4) 基于边缘覆盖概率或条件期望覆盖大小（固定候选顶点后的预期覆盖规模）对顶点排序，指导递归贪心归约；(5) 可扩展到低深度Trotter化，对有界度图电路深度与系统大小无关。

Result: 在多个随机图族上，使用独立标定集成设定行走时间，量子信息引导的算法相比经典贪心基线获得了更低的平均近似比，并能以更高比例找到最优解。条件能量策略在测试实例上表现最佳，且在低深度Trotter化下仍保持接近精确连续时间极限的算法性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明量子行走产生的约束保持概率信息可以有效增强经典贪心算法，且框架无需惩罚项和变分训练，具备硬件友好性（有界度图下Trotter层电路深度恒定），为混合量子-经典组合优化开辟了有前景的途径。

Abstract: We introduce a constraint-preserving hybrid quantum-classical greedy framework for the minimum vertex cover problem, which extends directly to maximum independent set by bitwise complementation.
