The framework uses projected Pauli-X terms whose sum preserves the feasible subspace and acts within it exactly as the adjacency matrix of a layered graph of feasible covers. This graph is connected, so every feasible cover is linked to the configuration containing all vertices by a sequence of allowed single-vertex flips. Starting from this configuration, the corresponding continuous-time quantum walk propagates amplitude into layers containing progressively smaller covers. We rank vertices using either their marginal cover probabilities or the expected cover size obtained after fixing each candidate vertex in the cover, and use these rankings to guide recursive greedy reductions. Across several random-graph families, with walk times fixed using independent calibration ensembles, the quantum-informed algorithms achieve lower mean approximation ratios and solve a larger fraction of instances optimally than their corresponding classical greedy baselines. The conditioned-energy strategy performs best on the tested instances and retains algorithmic performance close to the exact continuous-time limit under low-depth Trotterisation. For bounded-degree graphs, each Trotter layer has circuit depth independent of system size, and the framework requires neither penalty terms nor variational training.

</details>


### [145] [Design of a Quantum Error Correction Decoder Exploiting Temporal Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27930)
*Leo Itoh,Takuya Kasamura,Hidetsugu Irie,Junichiro Kadomoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于时间并行性的Sandwich解码微架构，使用Union-Find算法实现低延迟量子纠错，并通过ASIC实现验证，在码距d=21时相比传统批处理Union-Find解码器平均延迟降低35%，同时在唯象噪声下保持约1.5%的逻辑错误阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要低延迟的量子纠错(QEC)来防止综合征测量周期内错误的快速累积。传统批处理Union-Find解码器延迟较高，难以满足实时解码需求。

Method: 提出一种利用时间并行性的微架构，将Sandwich解码方法与Union-Find算法结合，并通过ASIC实现（逻辑综合与仿真验证）来评估性能。

Result: 在码距d=21时，平均延迟比传统批处理Union-Find解码器降低35%；在唯象噪声下逻辑错误阈值保持在约1.5%，与基线相当。

Conclusion: 所提出的时间并行Sandwich Union-Find解码微架构能在显著降低解码延迟的同时维持相当的纠错性能，为低延迟量子纠错提供了可行的ASIC实现方案。

Abstract: In the pursuit of fault-tolerant quantum computing, low-latency quantum error correction (QEC) is essential to prevent rapid error accumulation within the syndrome measurement cycle. In this work, we propose a microarchitecture that implements the sandwich decoding method using the Union-Find algorithm by exploiting temporal parallelism, and we present an ASIC implementation. Through logic synthesis and simulation, we show that the proposed decoder achieves an average latency reduction of 35% at code distance d = 21 compared with a conventional batch Union-Find decoder, while maintaining a comparable logical error threshold of approximately 1.5% under phenomenological noise.

</details>


### [146] [Complementary Matrix-Gated QKAN Fast-Weight Programmers for Quantum Dynamics Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27945)
*Kuo-Chung Peng,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Jiun-Cheng Jiang,Chen-Yu Liu,En-Jui Kuo,Yun-Yuan Wang,Tzung-Chi Huang,Prayag Tiwari,Chi-Sheng Chen,Chun-Hua Lin,Yu-Chao Hsu,Tai-Yue Li,Saif Al-Kuwari,Simon See,Kuan-Cheng Chen,Nan-Yow Chen,Hsi-Sheng Goan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种自调制量子启发KAN（QKAN）快速权重程序员（FWP）框架，用低秩生成的逐元素调制替代标量门控，并引入互补矩阵门控（CMG）实现逐坐标记忆控制，在保持有界凸更新和仿射前缀扫描结构的同时，显著提升了长序列预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列模型需要决定记忆中写入什么和保留什么。现有的QKAN-based FWP使用标量门控，对所有快速状态坐标施加相同的保留-写入平衡，迫使所有参数共享单一的记忆时间尺度，缺乏细粒度的记忆控制能力，限制了模型的表达力和预测性能。

Method: 作者提出了三种自调制规则：对新增提案分支、有界旧状态分支或两者同时应用低秩生成的逐元素调制。此外提出互补矩阵门控（CMG），使用一个sigmoid矩阵门保留旧状态，用其补矩阵写入新提案，实现逐坐标记忆控制，同时保持标量门控的有界凸更新和仿射前缀扫描结构，且只需单分支规则的调制头开销。

Result: 在四种FWP架构（组合经典与QKAN基慢/快程序员）上比较四种自调制规则，跨七个单步预测基准和五种序列长度，CMG对含QKAN模块的快程序员架构给出最一致的改进。在Jaynes-Cummings和transmon-resonator动力学的直接多步预测中（CUDA-Q Dynamics仿真），CMG模型在4、8、16步预测范围内保持均方误差0.001或更低，比标量门控对应模型至少改进91.2%。

Conclusion: 逐坐标互补调制被证实是QKAN-based FWP的一种稳定且有效的更新机制，为量子序列学习中的长期上下文建模提供了更优的记忆管理方案。

Abstract: Sequence models must decide what to write into memory and what to retain. In quantum and quantum-inspired sequence learning, nonlinear recurrent updates often require repeated circuit evaluations and sequential backpropagation through time, making long contexts costly. Gated fast-weight programmers (FWPs) based on quantum-inspired Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (QKANs) alleviate this bottleneck by storing context in time-varying fast parameters. However, their scalar gate applies one retention-write balance to every fast-state coordinate, forcing all parameters to share a memory timescale. We introduce Self-Modulating QKAN-based FWPs, which replace this broadcast gate with low-rank-generated element-wise modulation of the new-proposal branch, a bounded old-state branch, or both. We further propose Complementary Matrix Gating (CMG), which uses one sigmoid matrix gate to retain the old state and its complement to write the new proposal. CMG provides coordinate-wise memory control while preserving the bounded convex update and affine prefix-scan structure of scalar gating, at the modulation-head cost of a single-branch rule. We compare four self-modulating rules with scalar gating across four FWP architectures combining classical and QKAN-based slow and fast programmers. Across seven single-step forecasting benchmarks and five sequence lengths, CMG gives the most consistent improvements for architectures whose fast programmer incorporates a QKAN-based module. In direct multi-step forecasting of Jaynes-Cummings and transmon-resonator dynamics simulated with CUDA-Q Dynamics, CMG models maintain mean-squared errors on the order of 0.001 or lower across forecasting horizons of 4, 8, and 16 steps, while improving on their scalar-gated counterparts by at least 91.2%. These results establish coordinate-wise complementary modulation as a stable and effective update for QKAN-based FWPs.

</details>


### [147] [A CPU+DCU Heterogeneous Parallel Framework for Post-Processing Reconstruction in Quantum Circuit Cutting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27947)
*Qingqing Jiang,Weidong Liu,Yufu Liu,Ruiqing He,Jiandong Shang,Hengliang Guo,Qiang Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个CPU+DCU异构并行框架，用于量子电路切割后的经典后处理重建。该框架不从子电路测量结果构造稠密的2^n维概率向量，而是只重建原始输出分布中的非零概率状态，结合高/低字整数表示以支持超过64位的全局基态索引，并采用覆盖设备内存、主机内存和外存的三级协作存储机制。在嵩山超级计算机上的实验表明，该框架在保持高重建保真度的同时，对线性簇态实现高达259倍加速，对随机电路相对同构CPU并行方法实现最高4倍加速，并能完成百比特规模的重建任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NISQ时代量子比特资源有限，大型量子电路难以直接在真实硬件上执行。量子电路切割通过将大电路分解为小子电路来缓解这一限制，但将大量开销转移到经典后处理阶段。随着电路规模、复杂度和切割数量的增加，重建过程成为计算和存储的主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出CPU+DCU异构并行框架：(1)避免构造稠密2^n维概率向量，仅从子电路测量结果重建原始输出分布中的非零概率状态；(2)采用高/低字整数表示法支持超过64位的全局基态索引；(3)设计覆盖设备内存、主机内存和外存的三级协作存储机制。

Result: 在嵩山超级计算机上的实验表明：对线性簇态相对优化串行基线实现高达259倍加速；对随机电路相对同构CPU并行方法实现最高4倍加速；能够完成百比特规模的重建任务，同时保持高重建保真度。

Conclusion: 面向HPC的异构重建方法能有效缓解电路切割后的经典后处理瓶颈，并提高重建的可扩展性，为NISQ时代大规模量子电路执行提供了一条可行的技术路径。

Abstract: In the NISQ era, limited qubit resources make it difficult to execute large quantum circuits directly on real hardware. Quantum circuit cutting mitigates this limitation by decomposing a large circuit into smaller subcircuits, but it shifts substantial overhead to classical post-processing. As circuit size, complexity, and cut count increase, reconstruction becomes a major computational and storage bottleneck. This paper presents a CPU+DCU heterogeneous parallel framework for circuit-cutting post-processing reconstruction. Instead of constructing a dense $2^n$-dimensional probability vector or returning only high-probability states, the framework reconstructs the nonzero-probability states in the original output distribution from subcircuit measurement results. It combines heterogeneous CPU+DCU execution with a high/low-word integer representation for global basis-state indices beyond 64 bits and a three-level cooperative storage mechanism spanning device memory, host memory, and out-of-core storage. Experiments on the Songshan supercomputer show that the framework maintains high reconstruction fidelity while achieving up to $259\times$ speedup over an optimized serial baseline on linear-cluster states and up to $4\times$ speedup over a homogeneous CPU-parallel method on random circuits. The framework can also complete reconstruction tasks at the hundred-qubit scale. These results demonstrate that HPC-oriented heterogeneous reconstruction can effectively alleviate the classical post-processing bottleneck and improve reconstruction scalability.

</details>


### [148] [Exponential Advantage of Multipartite Entanglement over Quantum Communication with Applications to Bounded-Storage Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27957)
*Ananya Chakraborty,Manik Banik,Ronald de Wolf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个由多体量子纠缠实现的指数级通信优势。基于二分Hidden Matching问题，作者引入了多发送者-单接收者的通信任务，证明共享GHZ纠缠态可使每位发送者仅用对数比特经典通信完成任务；而无需预共享纠缠时，任何协议至少需要一位发送者进行多项式通信，即便允许量子通信也不行。此外，作为密码学应用，作者构造了种子双源随机性提取器，展示了纠缠与无纠缠量子侧信息之间的指数级分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子纠缠在通信复杂性中的优势，特别是多体纠缠（GHZ态）是否能带来超越二分纠缠的指数级通信提升，以及这种优势在密码学（随机性提取器）中的具体体现。

Method: (1) 基于二分Hidden Matching问题构造多发送者-单接收者的通信任务；(2) 证明共享GHZ态时每位发送者仅需O(log n)比特经典通信即可完成任务；(3) 通过下界证明，无预共享纠缠时任何协议（包括量子通信协议）需要多项式通信；(4) 将该通信任务转化为种子双源随机性提取器，分析纠缠与无纠缠量子侧信息下的提取能力差异。

Result: (1) 建立了多体纠缠的指数级通信优势：GHZ辅助的经典通信可指数级优于无纠缠的量子通信；(2) 构造了种子双源随机性提取器，在两种量子侧信息（纠缠与无纠缠）下表现出指数级分离——无纠缠时需多项式大小的量子记忆才能攻破，而存在少量共享纠缠时，指数级更小的量子记忆即可攻破。

Conclusion: 多体量子纠缠赋予了经典通信协议指数级的通信优势，这一优势不仅体现在通信复杂性理论上，还转化为密码学中随机性提取器的安全性分离。多体纠缠（而非仅二分纠缠）是实现这种指数级优势的关键资源。

Abstract: We establish an exponential communication advantage enabled by multipartite quantum entanglement. Building on the bipartite Hidden Matching problem, we introduce a communication task involving multiple spatially separated senders and a single receiver. We show that a shared Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger state enables completion of this task using only logarithmically many bits of classical communication from each sender. In contrast, without preshared entanglement, any protocol achieving high success probability requires polynomial communication from at least one sender, even when \emph{quantum} communication is allowed. Thus, classical communication assisted by multipartite entanglement can be exponentially more powerful than quantum communication without preshared entanglement. As a cryptographic application, we construct a seeded two-source randomness extractor and establish an exponential separation between entangled and unentangled quantum side-information. Specifically, compromising the extractor with two unentangled quantum states storing information about the two sources, respectively, requires polynomial-size memory, whereas exponentially smaller quantum memory suffices in the presence of a small amount of shared entanglement.

</details>


### [149] [Towards Quantum Networks: Characterizing Raman Noise over Metropolitan-scale Fiber Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27970)
*Marcello Caleffi,Laura d'Avossa,Italo Ignacio Machuca Flores,Marco Grillo,Elena Montella,Angela Sara Cacciapuoti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在真实城域光纤网络中分析了O波段经典信号在C波段产生的拉曼散射噪声对量子信号传输的影响，发现部署光纤中除拉曼散射外还存在局部频谱异常，并识别出受拉曼噪声影响较小的频谱区域，为量子信道分配提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子与经典信号在光纤基础设施中共存是大规模量子网络面临的主要挑战，拉曼散射等噪声源会严重影响纠缠分发及基于其构建的量子协议。现有研究多在受控实验室条件下进行，缺乏对真实电信网络环境（城域光纤）中拉曼散射效应的系统性分析。

Method: 在7公里城域光纤链路上使用商用光源和窄线宽激光器进行实验测量，分析O波段经典信号在C波段（用于纠缠分发）中产生的拉曼散射效应，并与实验室条件下的测量结果进行对比。

Result: 实验结果表明城域链路测量与实验室条件测量总体吻合，但在城域环路中观测到部署光纤的局部频谱异常。拉曼噪声在真实部署场景中对量子信噪比（SNR）构成支配性贡献，超过背景噪声和探测器噪声；同时识别出受拉曼噪声影响较小的频谱区域。

Conclusion: 当量子信号与O波段经典信道在同一光纤中同时传输时，需谨慎选择工作频率。研究结果为最优量子信道分配提供了实用指南，为在现有光纤网络上部署稳健、可扩展的量子通信系统提供了实际洞见。

Abstract: The coexistence of quantum and classical signals in optical fiber infrastructures represents a major challenge for large-scale quantum networks, as noise sources such as Raman scattering can significantly impact entanglement distribution, and the quantum protocols based on it. In this work, we analyze Raman scattering in the C-band, used for entanglement distribution, generated by a classical the O-band signal. The main contribution of this study lays in investigating these effects in a real metropolitan-fiber network, moving beyond controlled laboratory experiments to deployed telecommunication environments. Measurements are performed over a 7 km metropolitan fiber link using commercial sources and narrowband lasers. The experimental results shows good agreement between the measurements taken under laboratory conditions, although, within the metropolitan-scale loop, localized spectral anomalies are observed in the deployed fibers. Therefore, our results show that, whenever a quantum signal propagates in the C-band alongside an O-band classical channel within the same fiber, careful selection of the operating frequency is required, as Raman scattering and other real-world noise sources can significantly affect the quality and stability of the quantum transmission. In particular, we identify spectral regions that are less affected by Raman noise, thereby providing practical guidelines for optimal quantum channel allocation. We demonstrate that Raman-induced noise constitutes a dominant contribution to the quantum signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) in realistic deployments, beyond background and detector noise. Overall, our findings offer practical insights for deploying quantum communication systems over existing fiber networks, supporting the development of robust and scalable quantum infrastructures.

</details>


### [150] [Slow-light-enhanced Atomic Frequency Comb Quantum Memory in Stoichiometric EuCl$_3 \cdot$ 6D$_2$O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28060)
*Zongfeng Li,Wanting Xiao,Mucheng Guo,Shuping Liu,Fudong Wang,Manjin Zhong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在化学计量比EuCl₃·6D₂O晶体中实现了高效率量子存储，该晶体天然具备高光学密度，无需腔体增强。在强吸收高密度体系中观测到显著的慢光效应，包括色散诱导的回波延迟和精细度相关的回波强度调制。经典光存储效率达42.9%，弱相干脉冲达34.4%，慢光存储效率高达90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土掺杂晶体是量子存储的有力候选材料，但在自由空间构型中，其性能受到低光学深度的根本限制。传统解决方案（如光腔）增加系统复杂度，因此需要寻找一种无需腔体实现就能提供高光学密度的替代平台。

Method: 使用化学计量比EuCl₃·6D₂O晶体（天然高光学密度）实现量子存储；开发了统一的理论框架，阐明吸收和色散如何协同作用于回波生成机制；在实验上测量了存储效率和慢光效应。

Result: 经典光存储效率42.9%，弱相干脉冲存储效率34.4%，慢光存储效率90%；在实验中观测到色散诱导的回波延迟和精细度依赖的回波强度调制等慢光效应。

Conclusion: 验证了EuCl₃·6D₂O作为稳健量子存储平台的可行性，为可扩展固态量子存储器提供了有效路径，绕过了传统低光学深度对自由空间量子存储的根本限制。

Abstract: Rare-earth-doped crystals are promising candidates for quantum storage, yet their performance in free-space configurations is fundamentally restricted by low optical depth. Here, we demonstrate high-efficiency quantum storage in a stoichiometric EuCl$_3 \cdot$ 6D$_2$O crystal, which intrinsically provides high optical density without the complexity of cavity implementation. We show that in this high-density regime, the system exhibits significant slow-light-like effects, including dispersion-induced echo delays and finesse-dependent echo intensity modulation. We develop a unified theoretical framework showing how absorption and dispersion work in concert to mediate echo generation. We achieve storage efficiencies of 42.9% for classical light and 34.4% for weak coherent pulses, alongside 90% efficiency for slow-light storage. These findings validate EuCl$_3 \cdot$ 6D$_2$O as a robust platform, establishing a viable pathway for scalable solid-state quantum memory.

</details>


### [151] [Deterministic QKD source robust against side-channel attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28063)
*Kiyoshi Tamaki,Marcos Curty,Akihiro Mizutani,Álvaro Navarrete*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种内在抗侧信道攻击的量子密钥分发（QKD）光源方案，无需后选择、无需完美消光比器件，也不引入强度与编码比特/基之间的相关性，从而适用更简单且更高效的安全证明，可获得显著更高的密钥率，且在现有技术条件下可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QKD在原理上安全，但实际设备与安全证明中的理想模型存在偏差，会破坏实际安全性。源端侧信道（尤其是特洛伊木马攻击）尤为严重：忽略它们会危及实现安全性，而考虑它们又会降低性能。现有被动式无调制器方案要么需要脉冲后选择，要么需要具有完美消光比的器件来抑制侧信道，且可能引入强度与编码信息之间的相关性，均存在局限。

Method: 提出一种新型QKD光源，该光源对侧信道攻击具有内在鲁棒性。它既不需要对发射脉冲进行后选择，也不依赖具有完美消光比的器件来抑制侧信道，且设计上避免了强度与编码比特或基之间的相关性引入。因此可以直接应用更简单的安全证明框架。

Result: 该方案无需后选择和完美消光比器件即可实现内在的抗侧信道攻击，简化了安全证明，同时大幅提升了密钥率，并且与现有技术水平兼容，提供了迈向实现安全QKD的切实可行路径。

Conclusion: 该提案为实现实现安全的QKD指明了一条清晰且实用的路径，在保持抗侧信道攻击能力的同时显著提升密钥率，有望推动QKD从原理安全走向实际安全部署。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) is secure in principle, but practical security can be undermined by discrepancies between real devices and the idealized models assumed in security proofs. Source side channels, including those exploited by Trojan-horse attacks, are particularly detrimental: neglecting them compromises implementation security, whereas accounting for them reduces performance. Here we propose a QKD source that is intrinsically robust against side-channel attacks. Unlike existing passive and modulator-free schemes, it requires neither post-selection of the emitted pulses nor devices with a perfect extinction ratio to suppress side channels, and it does not introduce correlations between the intensity and the encoded bit or basis. Consequently, simpler security proofs apply directly, yielding substantially higher key rates. Our proposal appears to be within reach of current technology and therefore provides a clear and practical path toward implementation-secure QKD.

</details>


### [152] [Approximate sampling from decoded quantum interferometry via Markov chain Monte Carlo methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28120)
*Elies Gil-Fuster,Matan Ninio,Lennart Bittel,Yishai Shimoni,Jens Eisert,Stefan Woerner,Almudena Carrera Vázquez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了解码量子干涉仪（DQI）的实证性能，并探讨经典采样方法（特别是马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛/块吉布斯采样）能否模拟其优化能力。作者提出了DQI的简化解析表征，将其预期性能与二项分布统计关联，并在max-XORSAT（超过1000个有效量子比特）和OPI（超过150个有效量子比特）两个优化问题上评估了经典MCMC的运行时间尺度。结果表明MCMC能在广泛问题规模下可靠达到DQI的近似比；在OPI声称具有超多项式优势的区间，MCMC的实证运行时间约为1.1^n，即指数增益但底数较小。研究不否定现有量子优势声明，但提供了经典采样算法可紧密匹配DQI优化性能的新实证证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于DQI的研究主要聚焦于理论复杂性分析，对其相对于经典算法的实证性能知之甚少。作者旨在填补这一空白，探究经典采样方法能否在优化能力上模拟DQI，从而为评估DQI的实际优势提供更细致的视角。

Method: 作者首先给出了DQI的简化解析表征，将其预期性能与二项分布联系起来，并指出进一步研究DQI复杂性的具体障碍。利用DQI输出概率可高效计算的性质，采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）技术，特别是块吉布斯采样，从诱导分布中采样。在max-XORSAT和OPI两个优化问题上系统研究了运行时间尺度。

Result: MCMC算法在广泛问题规模下能可靠达到DQI的预期近似比。在max-XORSAT上超越1000个有效量子比特，在OPI上超越150个有效量子比特。在OPI中声称DQI具有超多项式优势的区域，MCMC的实证运行时间近似为1.1^n，即增长速度虽为指数但底数较小。

Conclusion: 该研究不否认现有量子优势声明，但提供了强有力且新颖的实证证据表明经典采样算法能紧密匹配DQI的优化性能。这一发现为DQI的实际（而非理论）优势提供了一个更为细致和审慎的视角。

Abstract: Optimization problems are among the leading candidates for industrially relevant quantum advantage. Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) has been proposed to tackle approximate optimization, establishing a connection to classical decoding problems. While previous work has primarily focused on the theoretical complexity of DQI, comparatively little is known about its empirical performance relative to classical algorithms. In this work, we shed further light on the complexity of DQI and investigate numerically whether classical sampling methods can emulate the optimization capabilities of DQI. We first present a simplified analytical characterization of DQI that connects its expected performance to binomial statistics, and we identify concrete obstacles in further studying the complexity of DQI. Exploiting the fact that DQI output probabilities are efficiently computable, we apply Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) techniques, particularly block-Gibbs sampling, to sample from the induced distribution. We study the runtime scaling of these methods for two optimization problems called max-XORSAT, where we reach beyond $1000$ effective qubits; and OPI, where we reach beyond $150$ effective qubits. Our results show that MCMC algorithms can reliably attain the approximation ratios expected from DQI across a broad range of problem sizes. In OPI, in the regime where a super-polynomial advantage is claimed for DQI, we observe an empirical runtime for MCMC that scales approximately as $1.1^{n}$, indicating exponential growth with a comparatively small base. Our findings do not refute existing quantum advantage claims but provide new empirical evidence that classical sampling algorithms can closely match DQI's optimization performance, offering a more nuanced perspective on the practical advantage of DQI.

</details>


### [153] [Efficient atom rearrangements for quantum error correction primitives with a single AOD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28149)
*Tom Hartweg,Asier Piñeiro Orioli,Hugo Perrin,Samuel Crew*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用单个动态声光偏转器（AOD）对的扫描，实现对静态晶格中二维原子阵列的剪切、旋转和反射等新原语，通过（负）二进制和几何分解方法，将AOD行程次数从O(d²)降低到对数级别，从而大幅缩短中性原子量子计算机中逻辑操作所需的原子重排时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子量子计算机通过原子输运实现任意连接性，部分逻辑操作可简化为原子的几何重排。最小化这些移动操作的持续时间对于提高逻辑吞吐量至关重要。传统的逐原子重排方法效率低下，需要更高效的原子阵列操作方案。

Method: 引入新的原语来剪切、旋转和反射静态晶格中的二维原子阵列，利用单个动态交叉声光偏转器（AOD）对的扫描实现；采用（负）二进制和几何分解（如Paeth分解）来实现AOD行程次数的对数级缩放。

Result: 在旋转表面码中实现90°旋转（用于横向Hadamard门）仅需3⌊log₂(d-1)⌋+4次AOD行程和O(d^{1/3})时间（恒定加速度），而逐原子重排需要O(d²)次行程和O(d^{7/3})时间。

Conclusion: 通过几何分解和（负）二进制编码，中性原子阵列的重排操作效率实现了从多项式到对数级别的巨大提升，显著改善了量子逻辑操作的吞吐量。

Abstract: Neutral-atom quantum computers offer arbitrary connectivity enabled by atom transport. Some logical operations can then be simplified or reduced entirely to geometric rearrangements of the atoms. Minimizing the duration of these movements is therefore essential for high logical throughput. We introduce new primitives to shear, rotate and reflect 2D arrays of atoms in a static lattice using sweeps of a single dynamic crossed acousto-optic deflector (AOD) pair. Using (nega-)binary and geometric decompositions, we achieve an AOD stroke count scaling logarithmically in the linear size of the array. In one example, we use the Paeth decomposition to implement a $90^{\circ}$ rotation for a transversal Hadamard gate in a rotated surface code of distance $d$ in $3\lfloor\log_2(d-1)\rfloor + 4$ AOD strokes and $O(d^{1/3})$ constant-jerk time, against $O(d^2)$ strokes and $O(d^{7/3})$ time for atom-by-atom rearrangement.

</details>


### [154] [Entanglement Swapping with Integrated Narrowband Photon Sources for Quantum Repeaters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28184)
*Mingsong Wu,Leili Esmaeilifar,Rui Ning Wang,Tobias Kippenberg,Rob Thew,Tiff Brydges*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文演示了利用集成光子源实现的纠缠交换，其光子带宽兼容多种原子基量子记忆平台。实验获得净HOM可见度0.99±0.01，净交换态可见度0.88±0.06（足以违反Bell不等式），并利用独立泵浦激光器（频率差达1.6 THz）模拟了不同量子中继节点间的纠缠交换，展示了全光纤商用组件的相位与频率稳定方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一代量子中继器的实际部署需要与光子源接口的原子基量子记忆系统。集成光子学为光纤部署提供了前景，但许多主流量子记忆平台需要窄带光子，而集成光子源难以产生窄带光子；同时窄带光子在纠缠交换中面临系统级稳定化的技术难题。本文旨在解决这些基础性与技术性挑战。

Method: 使用最先进的集成光子源产生与多种原子基量子记忆平台带宽兼容的窄带光子对；采用两个独立泵浦激光器（频率差异显著）分别驱动两个光子对源，模拟不同量子中继节点间的纠缠交换；利用全光纤商用组件实现跨1.6 THz范围的相位与频率稳定化。

Result: 背景扣除（净）HOM可见度为0.99±0.01，表明光子具有高不可区分性和纯度；净交换态可见度V=0.88±0.06，证明最终纠缠态足以违反Bell不等式。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了场部署量子中继器中的关键挑战：集成光子学方案生成窄带光子对，以及独立量子中继节点间的系统级稳定化。结果为实现基于原子基量子记忆的量子中继器实际部署提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: Promising implementations of first generation quantum repeaters are predicted to require atomic-based quantum memory systems interfaced with photonic sources. Integrated photonics provides a promising solution for fibre-based, field-deployed operation of quantum repeaters, however many leading quantum memory platforms require narrow-bandwidth photons that are challenging to generate with integrated photonics. Narrowband photons also present significant technical challenges when implementing entanglement-swapping, particularly with regards to systems-level stabilisation. This work addresses some of these fundamental and technical challenges, by demonstrating entanglement-swapping using state-of-the-art integrated photon sources with bandwidths compatible with multiple atomic-based quantum memory platforms. We obtained a background-subtracted (net) HOM visibility of 0.99$\,\pm\,$0.01, showing high photon indistinguishability and purity, with a net swapped state visibility of $\mathcal{V}$=0.88$\,\pm\,$0.06 demonstrating that the final entanglement would be sufficient to violate a Bell inequality. The experiment used independent pump lasers for each photon pair source, with highly different frequencies to mimic entanglement swapping between different repeater nodes or platforms. Phase and frequency stabilisation spanning 1.6 THz was achieved using all-fibre, commercially-available components. These results address important challenges in implementing field-deployed quantum repeaters, from the integrated photonic solutions for narrowband photon pairs, to systems-level stabilisation between independent quantum repeater nodes.

</details>


### [155] [Purifications for Convex Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28202)
*Felix Campidell,Tim Netzer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限维真凸锥几何中的纯化原理，证明了不可分解锥和包含最大纠缠态的中间张量锥的纯化存在性定理，并给出局部自同构下唯一性的判定准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯化原理在量子理论和广义概率论中具有基础性重要性，但已有研究多依赖于特定代数结构。本文希望仅利用凸锥的几何性质来研究纯化，从而为更广泛的物理理论提供统一的数学框架。

Method: 利用有限维真凸锥的几何结构（锥的边界、面、自同构等），结合张量锥构造，建立纯化的存在性与唯一性定理。关键方法包括：对不可分解锥证明存在性、通过中间张量锥构造包含最大纠缠态的纯化、利用局部自同构刻画唯一性、通过面是否为单纯形给出边界点的纯化判定。

Result: 证明了不可分解齐次锥的每个内点都允许纯化（如Lorentz锥），并给出唯一性准则；在边界上，若所有真面都是单纯形，则只有纯点才有纯化；反之存在非单纯形面时该结论失效。正半定锥、Lorentz锥、k-正映射、PPT张量和多面体锥的实例展示了存在性与非唯一性现象。

Conclusion: 纯化原理可以纯粹从凸锥几何的角度加以理解和刻画，其结果既推广了量子理论中的已知结论，也为广义概率论中更一般的理论体系提供了纯化的数学基础。

Abstract: Motivated by the importance of the purification principle in quantum theory and generalized probabilistic theories, we study purifications using only the geometry of a finite-dimensional proper convex cone. We prove an existence theorem for indecomposable cones and intermediate tensor cones containing the maximally entangled state; in particular, every interior point of an indecomposable homogeneous cone admits a purification. This applies to Lorentz cones, for example. We also give a criterion for uniqueness up to local automorphisms. On the boundary, we show that if every proper face of $C$ is simplicial, then only pure points can admit purifications, and we demonstrate that this conclusion fails in the presence of non-simplicial faces. Examples involving positive semidefinite cones, Lorentz cones, $k$-positive maps, PPT tensors, and polyhedral cones illustrate both existence and non-uniqueness phenomena.

</details>


### [156] [Quantum Arago-Fresnel interference of displaced spin states of photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28231)
*Yan Zhang,Xiang Liu,Hou Ian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视Arago-Fresnel干涉定律，利用非经典态的现代描述，研究了由光子螺旋度产生的宏观Arago-Fresnel干涉。通过计算由规则相干态和位移单光子自旋态组合而成的偏振光束的Stokes参数，发现自旋取向及其相对于相干态偏振方向的非对称性会产生可区分的参数依赖性和干涉条纹，从而建立了一种纯光学方法来确定未知入射光子的自旋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arago和Fresnel的四条定律通过两束光的共面性来判断其干涉能力，是理解光偏振概念的历史里程碑。作者希望利用现代非经典态的描述重新审视这一经典问题，将经典干涉定律推广到量子领域，并探索光子自旋（螺旋度）在干涉中的可观测效应。

Method: 作者计算了由两类量子态组合而成的偏振光束的Stokes参数：（1）规则相干态（从真空位移）；（2）位移单光子自旋态（从左旋或右旋光子自旋态位移）。通过分析自旋取向及其相对于位移相干态偏振方向的相对不对称性，研究它们如何产生不同的参数依赖性和干涉条纹。

Result: 自旋取向以及与位移相干态偏振方向的相对不对称性会产生可区分的参数依赖关系，从而形成不同的干涉条纹图案。这一量子干涉测量方法能够作为纯光学手段来确定未知入射光子的自旋状态。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典的Arago-Fresnel干涉定律推广到具有光子螺旋度的非经典态，建立了量子干涉与光子自旋测量之间的对应关系，提供了一种完全基于光学测量来确定光子自旋的新方法。

Abstract: The four laws by Arago and Fresnel distinguish the coplanarity of two light beams to determine their capacity of interference, laying the historic milestone for conceptualizing the polarization of light. Equipped with modern descriptions of non-classical states, we re-investigate the macroscopic Arago-Fresnel interference producible by photon helicities. To this end, we compute the Stokes parameter of a polarized beam combined from a regular coherent state (displaced from the vacuum) and a displaced single-photon spin state (displaced from either a left- or right-spin state of photon). The spin orientation, together with its relative asymmetry with respect to the polarizing orientation of the displacing coherent state, produces distinguishing parameter dependences and thus distict interference fringes. Conversely, this quantum interferometry establishes a purely optical method to determine the spin of an unknown incident photon.

</details>


### [157] [Optimal T Counts under Sparsity: from QROM to State Preparation and Block Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28260)
*Tongyang Li,Fengning Ou,Xinzhao Wang,Penghui Yao,Pei Yuan,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究稀疏QROM（量子只读存储器）的T计数复杂度，证明了在支持大小为s、消息长度为m的情况下，T计数的最优下界和上界均为Θ(√sm + √sn)，并由此推导出稀疏态制备和稀疏矩阵块编码的匹配T计数界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多量子算法需要相干地访问经典数据，通常建模为QROM。然而，现实场景中的数据往往是稀疏的（只有s个非零地址），直接应用稠密QROM的方案会浪费大量资源。此前缺乏对稀疏QROM的T计数复杂度的系统性研究，因此需要建立严格的上下界。

Method: 上界方面，作者提出了一种多层次哈希（multilevel hashing）方案来构造稀疏QROM，实现Θ(√sm + √sn)的T计数上界。下界方面，作者将稀疏QROM归约到态制备（state preparation）问题，并利用自适应Clifford+T电路的计数论证来证明下界。这些下界在允许中间测量和经典控制操作的情况下仍然成立。

Result: 证明了稀疏QROM的T计数渐近最优界为Θ(√sm + √sn)。作为应用，获得了s-稀疏态制备的匹配T计数界Θ(√sn + √s·log(1/ε) + log(1/ε))，以及s-稀疏矩阵块编码的T计数界Θ(√(2^n·sn) + √(2^n·s·log(s/ε_BE)) + log(s/ε_BE))。

Conclusion: 本文填补了稀疏QROM复杂度分析的空白，给出了严格且渐近最优的T计数上下界，其结论对稀疏态制备和稀疏矩阵块编码等关键量子算法子程序具有直接的实际指导意义。

Abstract: Many quantum algorithms require coherent access to classical data, often modeled by quantum read-only memory (QROM). We initiate the study of the $T$ count of sparse QROM, in which only $s$ of the $2^n$ addresses store nonzero data. We prove asymptotically optimal $T$-count bounds $Θ(\sqrt{sm} + \sqrt{sn})$ with square-root dependence on the support size $s$ and message length $m$. Our upper bounds use a multilevel hashing scheme, while our lower bounds reduce sparse QROM to state preparation and use counting arguments for adaptive Clifford+$T$ circuits. The lower bounds thus hold even when mid-circuit measurements and classically controlled operations are allowed. As applications, we obtain matching $T$-count bounds $Θ(\sqrt{sn} + \sqrt{s\log(1/\varepsilon)} +
  \log(1/\varepsilon))$ for $s$-sparse state preparation and $Θ( \sqrt{2^n sn}
  +
  \sqrt{2^n s\log(s/\varepsilon_{\mathrm{BE}})}
  +
  \log(s/\varepsilon_{\mathrm{BE}}))$ for block encoding of $s$-sparse matrices, where $\varepsilon$ and $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{BE}}$ are the precision of state preparation and block encoding, respectively.

</details>


### [158] [No-Go Theorems for Quantum Transport Metrics from Fixed Cost Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28358)
*Minbo Gao,Zhengfeng Ji,Tianshi Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在量子最优输运中，基于固定成本算子的耦合公式无法在完整的量子态空间上诱导出度量。对于标准耦合，在任意维度d≥3下，不存在任何固定成本算子能使最优成本或其平方根成为度量，且障碍在可交换态中就已出现；对于信道诱导的耦合，全局非负性和自成本为零迫使成本算子为零，导致d≥2时无法实现点分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Friedland等人于2022年推测SWAP投影算子的最优成本平方根在所有维度上都是度量，且该性质对邻近的量子成本矩阵仍然成立。Miller通过构造对角qutrit反例推翻了这个猜想。本文旨在推广Miller的分析，建立更一般的no-go定理，从而彻底澄清固定成本算子耦合公式的度量性质。

Method: 作者基于Miller对F=qutrit（维度3）量子系统构造的对角反例分析，将其推广到任意维度d≥3。通过分析标准耦合（standard couplings）下的最优成本性质，证明不存在固定成本算子使最优成本或其平方根满足度量公理（特别是三角不等式）。同时分别分析了SWAP成本的稳定化情形以及信道诱导耦合（channel-induced couplings）情形下的约束。

Result: 得到了统一的no-go定理：(1) 对任意d≥3，不存在固定成本算子能使标准耦合的最优成本或其平方根成为度量，违规在可交换态中已出现；(2) SWAP成本的稳定化不消除该障碍；(3) 对信道诱导耦合，全局非负性和自成本为零迫使成本算子为零，导致d≥2时无法满足点分离性质。这些结果证实固定成本耦合公式无法再完整量子态空间上诱导度量。

Conclusion: 固定成本耦合的量子最优输运公式在完备的量子态空间上本质上无法产生度量结构，因此需要寻找其他途径（如动态/最优控制公式或额外规范化处理）来定义量子态空间上的有意义的度量。

Abstract: Coupling-based quantum optimal transport generalizes classical optimal transport by representing transport plans as bipartite states with prescribed marginals and evaluating their cost as the expectation of a fixed Hermitian operator. Friedland et al. [Phys. Rev. Lett. 129, 110402 (2022)] conjectured that the square root of the optimal cost associated with the SWAP projector is a metric in every dimension and that this property persists for nearby quantum cost matrices. Miller [arXiv:2607.07764] disproved both conjectures by constructing explicit diagonal qutrit counterexamples. Building on his analysis, we prove a uniform no-go theorem for standard couplings. In every dimension $d\geq3$, no fixed cost operator makes either the optimal cost or its square root a metric, with violations occurring already among commuting states. The obstruction persists under stabilization of the SWAP cost. For channel-induced couplings, global nonnegativity and vanishing self-cost force the cost operator to be zero, precluding point separation when $d\geq2$. Taken together, these no-go results show that fixed-cost coupling formulations do not lead to metrics on the full quantum state space.

</details>


### [159] [Emergence of a Macroscopic Cat State and Multi-Channel Entanglement in a Frustrated Cluster Spin Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28373)
*Mohit Lal Bera,Andreu Anglés-Castillo,Alberto Acevedo Meléndez,Rafael Gómez-Lurbe,Luca Ion,Rodrigo M. Sanz,Somayeh Mehrabankar,Tanmoy Pandit,Carmen G. Almudéver,Armando Pérez,Miguel Angel Garcia-March*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维阻挫自旋链（结合cluster-Ising和反对称次近邻Ising模型），证明系统存在两个量子相，由一阶量子相变分隔：铁磁相具有宏观猫态和热力学极限下闭合的小能隙；非公度相保持大的非公度量子关联和有能隙，但在Schmidt系数上表现为四个主要分量（对应四条二分纠缠通道），且不再建立拓扑序。文章还讨论了宏观猫态在量子计量学中的应用及实验可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在理解阻挫自旋链中竞争相互作用如何影响量子相的性质，特别是澄清一个常见的误解——竞争相互作用并不总能产生拓扑相，而可能产生一种本质上不同于简单顺磁态的非公度相，并量化其量子关联特征。

Method: 从历史视角论证所研究模型的合理性，随后通过解析和数值手段详细分析系统的基态性质，包括能隙行为（热力学极限下的闭合与否）、Schmidt系数分解、二分纠缠通道的计数，以及相变阶次的判定。

Result: 证明两个量子相被一阶量子相变分开：铁磁相具有宏观猫态、小能隙（热力学极限闭合）和两个主导Schmidt系数；非公度相为有能隙相（热力学极限下能隙不闭合），具有大的非公度量子关联，但有四个主导Schmidt系数，对应四条实质性贡献的二分纠缠通道，并证明其与简单顺磁态根本不同。

Conclusion: 竞争相互作用的引入避免了拓扑相的建立，却产生了非公度相这一新的量子物态；铁磁相中的宏观猫态对量子计量学有潜在应用价值，且系统具有实验可实现性。

Abstract: We study a one-dimensional frustrated spin chain, which combines cluster-Ising and anisotropic next-nearest neighbor Ising models. We first offer a historical perspective that justifies the studied model. Then we study in detail the two quantum phases and prove that they are separated by a first order quantum phase transition. On one side, the ground state corresponds to a ferromagnetic phase, shows the presence of macroscopic cat states, and a small gap that closes in the thermodynamic limit. On the other phase, competing interactions avoid the establishment of a topological phase, though it conserves large incommensurate quantum correlations. We prove it is fundamentally distinct from a simple paramagnet, and we name it an incommensurate phase. This is a gapped phase, which gap does not close in the thermodynamic limit. While in the ferromagnetic phase there are two dominant Schmidt coefficients, in the incommensurate phase there are four. This corresponds to four distinct bipartite entanglement channels contributing substantially to the ground state. Finally, we discuss the utility of the macroscopic cat states for quantum metrology applications and the experimental feasibility of the system.

</details>


### [160] [Counterdiabatic Driving under Variational Frame Dressing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28383)
*Boxi Li,Franco Nori,Felix Motzoi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在变分缀饰框架下构造可实现的逆绝热（CD）驱动协议的通用形式体系，通过将无约束的辅助生成元与实验可操作的本地控制场相映射，解决了传统CD驱动无法对应物理可操作场的问题，并在多比特链消隐噪声、Bell态制备加速和全息门实现等场景中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆绝热（Counterdiabatic）驱动虽然能够加速绝热协议，但其辅助控制场往往无法映射到实验上真实可用的物理操作上，导致理论协议在实际系统中不可实现，因此需要一种系统性的框架来保证CD协议的可实现性。

Method: 作者在变分缀饰框架（variational dressed frame）中重新表述CD驱动：无约束的辅助生成元同时重塑有效绝热问题，并强制所施加的修正约束在本地（native）实验室控制场内。由此得到实验室坐标系下的对易子方程，该方程无需构造瞬时本征态或缀饰框架酉变换即可求解。缀饰框架提供的额外自由度揭示了传统绝热框架中不可达的解。

Result: 在三个场景中验证了该框架的有效性：（1）在单正交相位驱动的多比特链中抑制旁观者（spectator）误差；（2）利用固定纠缠相互作用实现Bell态制备的解析加速；（3）在简并三脚架（tripod）流形中，以约束在本征耦合上的修正脉冲实现快速绝热全息门。

Conclusion: 该工作为非微扰的、可实现的逆绝热协议提供了系统性构造框架，突破了传统绝热框架的限制，为量子控制中加速绝热过程提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Counterdiabatic (CD) driving accelerates adiabatic protocols by prescribing auxiliary control fields, but often fails to map them to physically available operations. We derive a general formalism for such a mapping. We formulate CD driving in a variational dressed frame, where an unconstrained auxiliary generator reshapes the effective adiabatic problem while simultaneously forcing the applied correction to stay restricted to the native laboratory controls. This yields a laboratory-frame commutator equation that can be solved without constructing instantaneous eigenstates or the adiabatic and dressed-frame unitaries. The additional dressed-frame freedom reveals solutions that are inaccessible in the conventional adiabatic frame. We illustrate this mechanism in three settings: suppression of spectator errors in a multi-qubit chain driven by a single quadrature, an analytical acceleration of adiabatic Bell-state preparation with a fixed entangling interaction, and implementation of a fast adiabatic holonomic gate in a degenerate tripod manifold with correction pulses confined to the native couplings. Our results provide a systematic nonperturbative framework for constructing implementable counterdiabatic protocols beyond the conventional adiabatic frame.

</details>


### [161] [Nearly optimal quantum circuits for Boolean oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28402)
*Junhong Nie,Wei Zi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对三类布尔函数的量子预言机（一般全函数、部分函数、稀疏函数）提出了电路尺寸、深度与辅助比特数之间近乎最优的折衷方案，所有尺寸和深度边界在相应辅助比特数区间内渐进最优（相差对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布尔函数的量子预言机是经典算法与量子算法之间的核心桥梁，但针对此类预言机的量子电路优化研究尚未闭环。作者希望填补这一空白，并为需要将经典过程嵌入量子电路的场景（如QROM实现和量子算法设计）提供支持。

Method: 对三类布尔函数分别给出折衷关系：1) 一般全布尔函数（输出规模b，辅助比特1到Θ(2^n/n)），尺寸O(b2^n/log(n+m))，深度O(b2^n/(n+m))；2) 部分布尔函数（有效支持规模d，辅助比特Θ(log d)到Θ(d)），尺寸O(n log d+bd)，深度O(n log n log d/(n+m)+log n+d(log d+b log m)/m)；3) 稀疏布尔函数（真实输入规模d，辅助比特Θ(log n+log d)到Θ(nd/log d)），尺寸O(n²log d+nd/log(log d+m/n))，深度O(n²log n log d/(n+m)+log n+nd/m)。

Result: 所有给出的尺寸和深度边界在相应的辅助比特数区间内都是渐进最优的，仅相差对数因子。

Conclusion: 这些接近最优的折衷结果有望应用于需要将经典过程嵌入量子电路的场景，如QROM实现和量子算法设计。

Abstract: Quantum oracle of Boolean functions is one of the central bridges between classical and quantum algorithms, but the study focusing at quantum circuit optimization of such oracle is yet closed. In this paper, we propose nearly optimal tradeoffs among circuit size, circuit depth and ancilla count, for quantum oracles of three kinds of Boolean functions:
  general total Boolean functions with output size $b$: with $1\le m\leΘ\left(\frac{2^n}{n}\right)$ ancilla, size $\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{b2^n}{\log(n+m)}\right)$, depth $\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{b2^n}{n+m}\right)$;
  partial Boolean functions of effective support size $d$ and output size $b$: with $Θ\left(\log d\right)\le m\le Θ\left(d\right)$ ancilla, size $\mathcal{O}\left(n\log d+bd\right)$, depth $\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{n\log n\log d}{n+m}+\log n+\frac{d(\log d+b\log m)}{m}\right)$;
  sparse Boolean functions of true input size $d$: with $Θ\left(\log n+\log d\right)\le m\leΘ\left(\frac{nd}{\log d}\right)$ ancilla, size $\mathcal{O}\left(n^2\log d+\frac{nd}{\log(\log d+m/n)}\right)$, depth $\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{n^2\log n\log d}{n+m}+\log n+\frac{nd}{m}\right)$.
  All the size and depth bounds are asymptotically optimal up to logarithmic factors in the corresponding ancilla count regions. We hope these results find applications in scenarios where classical procedures are needed to be embedded into quantum circuits, such as QROM implementation and quantum algorithm design.

</details>


### [162] [Improved Convergence of Carleman-Embedded Quantum Algorithm for the Vlasov-Poisson System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28426)
*Matthew Christensen,Tom Goffrey,Animesh Datta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将Carleman嵌入量子算法求解Vlasov-Poisson方程的收敛范围扩展到动力学等离子体物理中物理上合理的碰撞频率，通过Fourier-Hermite展开平移后的相空间分布函数，并用解析和数值下界证明了收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carleman嵌入量子算法此前在求解Vlasov-Poisson方程时收敛范围有限，需要扩展到物理上合理的碰撞频率，以验证该量子算法在实际等离子体物理问题中的适用性。

Method: 采用Fourier-Hermite展开平移后的相空间分布函数，建立Carleman嵌入的Vlasov-Poisson系统，并通过解析下界和数值下界两种方式分析收敛条件。同时考察不同基函数类别下的收敛性要求，以及时间平均与时间分辨输出对算法复杂度的影响。

Result: 证明了在物理上合理的碰撞频率下算法能够收敛；揭示了一大类基函数下，Carleman嵌入的Vlasov-Poisson系统收敛需要碰撞频率强度随速度分辨率增加而增大；量子算法的复杂度对时间平均输出与时间分辨输出有显著不同的依赖。

Conclusion: Carleman嵌入量子算法在更广泛的物理参数范围内对Vlasov-Poisson方程是可行的，但收敛条件对速度分辨率和基函数选择敏感，且输出类型（时间平均vs时间分辨）显著影响算法复杂度。

Abstract: We extend the regime of convergence of Carleman-embedded quantum algorithms that solve the Vlasov-Poisson equations from kinetic plasma physics. We establish convergence, using both analytical and numerical lower bounds, for physically reasonable collision frequencies using a Fourier-Hermite expansion of the shifted phase-space distribution function. We also show that for a large class of basis functions, the convergence of the Carleman-embedded Vlasov-Poisson system requires increasing collision frequency strength with velocity resolution. The complexity of the quantum algorithm depends strongly on whether we seek time-averaged or -resolved outputs.

</details>


### [163] [Classical Tensor Network and Quantum Fourier Transform Approaches for Large-Scale Carr-Madan Option Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28435)
*Sascha Hauck,Ivica Turkalj*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量网络的 Carr-Madan 期权定价框架，利用超快速傅里叶变换（SFFT）——量子傅里叶变换（QFT）的压缩张量列表示——来替代传统 FFT，避免了指数级内存需求，实现了亚指数计算复杂度的欧式期权定价，并在量子模拟器和量子硬件上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的基于傅里叶变换的欧式期权定价方法（如 Carr-Madan 框架）在特征函数可用时被广泛使用，但随着离散化网格不断细化，传统傅里叶变换的内存需求呈快速增长，成为大规模定价问题的计算瓶颈。

Method: 将 Carr-Madan 定价框架重构为张量网络形式，采用 SFFT（量子傅里叶变换 QFT 的压缩张量列/Tensor Train 表示）直接应用于张量化期权定价，避免显式构造指数规模的向量和傅里叶算子；同时通过 QFT 实现经典张量网络算法与量子算法之间的直接对比。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的 SFFT 方法在维持欧式看涨期权定价精度的同时，显著降低内存需求，计算复杂度呈亚指数增长；量子模拟与硬件执行结果进一步表明，经典张量网络方法和 QFT 量子方法均能避免传统傅里叶实现的指数级扩展，为大规模期权定价提供互补视角。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了经典傅里叶定价、张量网络算法与量子计算方法之间的统一框架，证明张量化傅里叶方法可为高维金融计算提供可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Fourier-based methods are among the most widely used techniques for pricing European options when the characteristic function of the underlying asset process is available. Their applicability to increasingly fine discretizations, however, is limited by the rapidly growing memory requirements of classical Fourier transforms, which become a computational bottleneck for large-scale pricing problems.
  In this work, we overcome this limitation by reformulating the Carr-Madan pricing framework using tensor networks. Specifically, we employ the Superfast Fourier Transform (SFFT), a compressed Tensor Train representation of the Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT), and apply it directly to tensorized option pricing without ever explicitly constructing exponentially large vectors or Fourier operators. This formulation also enables a direct comparison between the classical tensor network algorithm and its quantum counterpart through QFT-based option pricing on quantum simulators and quantum hardware. Numerical experiments for European call options demonstrate that the proposed SFFT method maintains pricing accuracy while substantially reducing memory requirements and achieving subexponential computational scaling compared with conventional FFT-based pricing. The accompanying quantum simulations and hardware executions enable a direct comparison between the classical tensor network formulation and its QFT-based quantum counterpart, showing that both approaches avoid the exponential scaling of conventional Fourier implementations and provide complementary perspectives on large-scale option pricing.
  Together, these results establish a unified framework connecting classical Fourier pricing, tensor network algorithms, and quantum computing approaches, demonstrating how tensorized Fourier methods can provide scalable alternatives for high-dimensional financial computations.

</details>


### [164] [Quantum Steering and Nonlocal Correlations Between Non-Interacting Delocalized Electrons Under Rashba Spin-Orbit Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28450)
*Zakaria Bouafia,Mostafa Mansour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维电子气中两个电子之间的量子导引和非局域关联，作为Rashba自旋轨道耦合强度和电子间距离的函数。研究发现，尽管增加RSOI最初会抑制量子关联，但所有三种度量（Bell非局域性、不确定性诱导非局域性和导引）均表现出非单调恢复，在最优耦合强度附近达到峰值，确立RSOI为稳定二维电子气量子性质的关键控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二维电子气中Rashba自旋轨道耦合作为控制参数，用于调控和稳定电子间量子关联（如量子导引和Bell非局域性），特别是在强RSOI系统Bi/Ag(111)中，理解量子关联随RSOI强度和电子间距的演化规律。

Method: 以Bi/Ag(111)系统为研究对象（其RSOI强度α₀=3.05×10⁻¹¹ eV·m），通过调节RSOI强度和电子间距离，计算并分析三类量子关联度量：Bell非局域性、不确定性诱导非局域性和量子导引的动力学行为。

Result: 增加α_R最初抑制量子关联，但所有三种度量均随α_R表现出非单调恢复，在最优耦合强度α_R=4.32×10⁻¹¹ eV·m附近达到峰值，且该现象在所考虑的电子间距离范围内普遍存在。

Conclusion: RSOI可作为二维电子气中稳定量子性质的关键控制参数，通过调节电子间距离和微调RSOI强度，可以有效控制Bi/Ag(111)系统中量子资源的抑制与恢复过程。

Abstract: We investigate quantum steering and nonlocal correlations between two electrons in a two-dimensional electron gas (2DEG) as functions of Rashba spin-orbit interaction (RSOI) strength and inter-electron separation. We focus particularly on the Bi/Ag(111) system characterized by its strong RSOI ($α_0 = 3.05\times10^{-11}$ eV~m), and we explore the influence of tuning intensity of RSOI and inter-electron distance on the dynamics of Bell nonlocality, uncertainty-induced nonlocality and steering. We find that, although increasing $α_R$ initially suppresses quantum correlations, all three metrics exhibit a non-monotonic recovery as functions of $α_R$, peaking near an optimal coupling strength $α_R = 4.32\times10^{-11}$~eV~m across the range of inter-electron separations considered. This finding establishes RSOI as a critical control parameter for stabilizing quantum properties in two-dimensional electron gases against the decay of quantum correlations with inter-electron separation, and shows that the suppression and recovery of quantum resources within the Bi/Ag(111) system can be controlled by adjusting the inter-electron distance and carefully tuning the RSOI strength.

</details>


### [165] [Optical linewidth narrowing for device-coupled single T centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28485)
*Adam Johnston,Yu-En Wong,Shengbin Yan,Ulises Felix-Rendon,Songtao Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过激光扫描显微镜的带隙以上光激发，成功将器件耦合的单个硅T中心的光学线宽缩小了高达70%，并将其归因于光生自由载流子填充附近电荷陷阱的机制。通过脉冲激发研究电荷稳定化动力学，并开发了速率方程模型来描述线宽变窄和中心位移现象，为推进T中心量子网络平台扫清了一个主要障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅中的单T中心是有前景的自旋光学量子比特平台，但谱扩散导致其光学线宽比腔增强辐射线宽大两个数量级，严重阻碍了该系统的量子网络应用。解决这一线宽问题是将T中心推向实用化量子信息处理的关键前提。

Method: 采用激光扫描显微镜提供带隙以上的光激发，作用于器件耦合的单个T中心；利用脉冲带隙以上激发分析电荷稳定化动力学，并构建速率方程模型来描述和解释所观测到的线宽变窄及中心频移现象。

Result: 实现了高达70%的光学线宽缩减，并确认了线宽变窄的物理机制是光生自由载流子填充附近的电荷陷阱，从而抑制了谱扩散引起的频移抖动。速率方程模型成功描述了电荷稳定化动力学及伴随的线宽变窄和中心位移行为。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种有效控制并降低单T中心光学线宽的方案，消除/扫清了推进T中心自旋平台在量子信息与量子网络应用中发展的主要障碍之一。

Abstract: Single T centers in silicon have emerged as promising optically active spins for quantum networking applications. One of the major obstacles to advancing the system is their broad optical linewidth due to spectral diffusion, which is two orders of magnitude larger than their cavity-enhanced radiative linewidth. We tackle this issue by utilizing above-band optical excitation delivered via a laser scanning microscope to device-coupled single T centers, achieving up to 70% optical linewidth reduction. We attribute the linewidth narrowing effect to the filling of nearby charge traps by photo-generated free carriers. We analyze charge stabilization dynamics by exploiting pulsed above-band excitation and develop a rate equation model to describe the dynamics and to explain the observed linewidth narrowing and center shift. This work provides an effective pathway to control and reduce the optical linewidth for single T centers, clearing one of the major roadblocks to advance the single T center spin platform for quantum information and networking applications.

</details>


### [166] [Generating broadband optical squeezing via Cascaded Micro-Ring Resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28493)
*Chung-Hsien Wang,Tian Zhong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在集成光子平台上生成宽带压缩真空的可扩展方案：通过级联的参量微环谐振器耦合到公共总线波导，克服了传统单腔源在压缩深度与光谱带宽之间的权衡。该架构在现实泵浦衰减条件下仍能产生宽而平坦的压缩光谱，对制造缺陷具有鲁棒性，且仅需N=5个耦合谐振器即可将所需带宽减少到单腔的四分之一，为工程化压缩储层提供了实用途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽带压缩光作为马尔可夫储层可指数级增强光与物质的相互作用，但传统单腔源面临压缩深度与光谱带宽之间不可调和的矛盾（trade-off）。这一根本限制阻碍了宽带压缩光源在量子技术中的实际应用，亟需新的架构设计来同时实现深度压缩与宽带宽。

Method: 提出级联参量微环谐振器阵列耦合到公共总线波导的架构。通过对输出光的分析，确定产生宽而平坦的压缩光谱的具体条件；在考虑腔内泵浦衰减等现实因素下优化设计；并通过数值验证考察对制造缺陷（如谐振频率不均、组件失效）的鲁棒性；比较平坦光谱与单腔洛伦兹谱在收敛到马尔可夫极限方面的差异。

Result: 该架构能产生宽而平坦的压缩光谱，对制造缺陷（频率不均匀和组件失效）具有鲁棒性。平坦光谱向马尔可夫极限收敛的速度远快于单腔洛伦兹轮廓。仅N=5个耦合谐振器、内禀损耗比κ_I/κ=0.1时，所需带宽仅为单腔达到相同压缩度所需带宽的四分之一。

Conclusion: 该级联谐振器架构通过将压缩过程分布到多个中等泵浦的谐振器上，显著降低了单宽带腔对低Q值和高增益的严苛要求，为在成熟的集成光子平台上实用化构建压缩储层提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Broadband squeezed light functioning as a Markovian reservoir can exponentially enhance light-matter interactions, benefiting quantum technologies. However, conventional single-cavity sources face a trade-off between squeezing depth and spectral bandwidth. We propose a scalable scheme for generating broadband squeezed vacuum using a cascade of parametric microring resonators coupled to a common bus waveguide. By analyzing the output, we identify the specific conditions that yield a broad, flat-topped squeezing spectrum, even under realistic intracavity pump attenuation. We demonstrate that this architecture is robust against fabrication imperfections, including inhomogeneous resonator frequencies and component failures. We show that the flat-topped spectrum converges to the Markovian limit significantly faster than a single-cavity Lorentzian profile. An array of as few as $N=5$ coupled resonators with an intrinsic loss ratio of $κ_I/κ= 0.1$ reduces the required bandwidth to a quarter of that needed by a single cavity to achieve same squeezing. This rapid convergence relaxes the low-$Q$ and high-gain constraints of single broadband cavities, distributing the squeezing process across moderately pumped resonators to provide a practical route for engineering squeezed reservoirs on mature integrated photonic platforms.

</details>


### [167] [Structure-Preserving Quantum Simulation of Wave Equations on a Trapped-Ion Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28499)
*Abhishek Shringi,Hsuan-Cheng Wu,Ahmed Shokry,Xiantao Li,Mahmut Taylan Kandemir*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Quantinuum H2-2捕获离子处理器上实现了基于傅里叶变换的保结构量子电路，用于一维和二维声波方程及变质量Dirac动力学的模拟，编码状态空间维度最高达4096，通过直接估计子域动能而非重建全波形，平均绝对误差在5.9×10⁻³至2.4×10⁻²之间，提供了当前硬件上可解析数千自由度的可观测量动力学证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波动方程为近中期量子模拟偏微分方程提供了天然测试平台，但现有硬件演示在空间维度、方程类别、系统规模和物理意义输出方面均存在局限，需要探索更大规模和更高维度上的可行性验证。

Method: 设计并基准测试基于傅里叶变换的保结构量子电路，用于一维和二维声波方程及变质量Dirac动力学。实验采用1×1024点和32×32二维网格（编码状态空间维度达4096），直接从测量样本中估计子域动能而非重建完整场。

Result: 在Quantinuum H2-2处理器上，所有声波和Dirac动力学测试中，实验结果与经典动能动力学轨迹的均绝对误差介于5.9×10⁻³和2.4×10⁻²之间。固定保留带宽下编译门数随网格量子比特数近似二次方增长；声波电路规模与演化时间基本无关，而Dirac电路成本随乘积公式步数增加。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在当今捕获离子处理器上，结构化波动问题的可观测量动力学可以在数千编码自由度上保持可解析性，为近中期量子计算在PDE模拟中的实用价值提供了硬件级证据。

Abstract: Wave equations provide a natural testbed for near-term quantum simulation of partial differential equations, but hardware demonstrations have remained limited in spatial dimension, equation class, system size, and physically meaningful output. We develop and benchmark structure-preserving, Fourier-based quantum circuits for the one- and two dimensional acoustic wave equations and Dirac dynamics with variable mass on the Quantinuum H2-2 trapped-ion processor. The experiments include one-dimensional grids with up to \(1024\) points and \(32\times32\) two-dimensional grids, corresponding to an encoded state-space dimension of up to \(4096\). Rather than reconstructing the full fields, we estimate subdomain kinetic energies directly from measurement samples. Across all tested acoustic and Dirac dynamics problems, the H2-2 results track the classical kinetic-energy dynamics with mean absolute errors between \(5.9\times10^{-3}\) and \(2.4\times10^{-2}\). At fixed retained bandwidth, the compiled gate counts grow approximately quadratically with the number of grid qubits; the acoustic circuit sizes are essentially independent of evolution time, whereas the cost also grows with the number of product-formula steps. These results provide hardware-level evidence that accurate observable dynamics can remain resolvable for structured wave problems with thousands of encoded degrees of freedom on a present-day trapped-ion processor.

</details>


### [168] [Spacetime Layout and Logical Compilation of Color Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28504)
*Qinjing Yu,Ke Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向颜色码的逻辑编译框架，基于其拓扑结构与任意子凝聚/畴壁性质，建立时空块图表示，并通过与ZX图的对应关系实现逻辑语义识别与变换，最终实现从ZX逻辑计算表示到颜色码时空布局的自动化编译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要在系统级协调逻辑原语，而颜色码虽具备通用逻辑操作能力，但缺乏从顶层逻辑计算到具体硬件布局的系统性编译方法，难以实现全栈量子计算。

Method: 1) 基于颜色码的任意子凝聚和畴壁结构，引入时空块图表示逻辑补丁和操作，推导块组装规则；2) 建立与ZX图的对应关系，识别表示的逻辑语义并支持保持计算语义的变换；3) 提出代码派生的编译策略，使用边装饰的ZX图适配颜色码的块组装约束，并通过融合区域感知路由利用语义等价性进行几何嵌入；4) 自动化整个逻辑编译流程。

Result: 成功自动化完成了完整的逻辑编译过程，并在广泛算法范围内验证了编译成功性，展示了颜色码从单个逻辑原语到逻辑计算自动综合的进步。

Conclusion: 该工作将颜色码架构从单一逻辑原语推进到逻辑计算的自动化综合，是全栈容错量子计算的重要一步。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires system-level coordination of logical primitives. Here, we establish a logical compilation framework for the color code, grounded in its topological structure and supporting universal logical operations. Based on its anyon-condensation and domain-wall structure, we introduce a spacetime block-diagram representation capturing logical patches and operations and derive the rules governing block assembly. A correspondence with ZX diagrams further identifies the logical semantics of this representation and enables transformations that preserve the represented computation. Moreover, we develop a code-derived compilation strategy that converts ZX representations of logical computations into valid color-code spacetime layouts. In this strategy, edge-decorated ZX diagrams tailor the logical representation to the color code under the block-assembly constraints, and fusion-region-aware routing exploits semantic equivalence during geometric embedding. We automate the complete logical compilation process and demonstrate successful compilation across a broad range of algorithms. Our work advances color-code architecture from individual primitives to the automated synthesis of logical computations, marking a significant step toward its full-stack quantum computing.

</details>


### [169] [When quantum thermal states look classical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28536)
*Harald Putterman,Alexander Zlokapa,Jordan Cotler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了高温量子Gibbs态在有限温度下的经典性质（无纠缠、无魔法、配分函数解析性、关联衰减、算法可解性），证明这些性质在系统尺寸无关的有限温度下仍然成立，但会在不同的逆温尺度上逐一失效，形成经典-量子转变的层级结构。研究涵盖了长程Pauli相互作用，解决了多个开放问题，并给出了多项式时间的经典Gibbs态制备算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温下量子Gibbs态呈现许多与最大混合态相似的经典特征，但此前不清楚这些特征在降温过程中如何以及何时失效。作者希望精确刻画这些经典性质持续存在的温度范围，并建立它们失效的临界温度层级，从而深化对量子纠缠、魔力和计算复杂度相变的理解。

Method: 针对具有有界强度的长程Pauli相互作用系统，通过严格分析证明纠缠死亡发生在恒定温度下（与系统尺寸无关），并给出紧致边界。同时利用热力学无限高温相的概念，证明在更冷的渐进温度下Gibbs态仍处于经典相，结合多项式时间的经典算法来制备Gibbs态和估计热期望。

Result: 1) 解决了Rouze、Franca和Alhambra（STOC'25）关于纠缠在恒定温度死亡的开放问题；2) 给出了多项式时间经典算法，可制备直到纠缠死亡转变温度的Gibbs态，该温度渐近地低于量子Gibbs采样器已知快速混合的温度，且改进了Bakshi等人（FOCS'24）的可分离温度至紧界；3) 证明了在渐近更冷的温度下Gibbs态仍处于热力学无限高温相，从而获得多项式时间经典算法估计含纠缠和魔力的热期望；4) 解决了Harrow、Mehraban和Soleimanifar（STOC'20）提出的关联衰减猜想。

Conclusion: 本文建立了量子Gibbs态经典特征失效的温度层级结构：无纠缠性质在恒定温度保持（紧界解析），无魔法等性质在更冷温度下仍可能持续。这项工作不仅在理论上给出了多个紧致边界并解决了若干开放问题，还将经典算法可处理Gibbs态的温度范围显著扩展，对量子热态制备与采样领域具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: At high temperature, quantum Gibbs states retain several classical features of the maximally mixed state: the absence of entanglement, the absence of magic, analyticity of the partition function, correlation decay, and algorithmic tractability. We prove new and sharp bounds showing that these features persist down to finite temperatures independent of system size, but fail at distinct inverse-temperature scales, forming a hierarchy of classical-to-quantum transitions. Our results hold for long-range Pauli interactions with bounded strength at every site. Despite such all-to-all interactions, we show that the death of entanglement occurs at constant temperature, resolving an open question of Rouze, Franca and Alhambra (STOC'25). We give a polynomial-time classical algorithm that prepares Gibbs states up to the death of entanglement transition. Notably, this is asymptotically colder than temperatures at which quantum Gibbs samplers are known to mix quickly, as well as the original separability temperature of Bakshi et al. (FOCS'24), which we improve to be tight up to constants. At asymptotically even colder temperatures, we show that the Gibbs state remains in the thermodynamic infinite-temperature phase. This leads to polynomial-time classical algorithms for estimating thermal expectations despite both entanglement and magic, and the resolution of a correlation decay conjecture of Harrow, Mehraban and Soleimanifar (STOC'20).

</details>


### [170] [A Provable Oracle-Free Quantum Algorithm for Nonlinear Dynamics on Hybrid Oscillator-Qubit Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28541)
*Kausthubh Chandramouli,Yan Li,Yuan Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合量子比特-量子连续模式（qubit-qumode）算法，用于求解漂移为多项式度L的非线性常微分方程组。算法沿Fokker-Planck路径传播状态密度，并通过warped-phase变换将离散生成元转化为参数化的薛定谔方程族，将Fourier模参数放置在单一连续变量量子模式上。核心结构结果是离散生成元的厄米部分允许二分Pauli分解，将非零Pauli串排序为O(log N)个互易交换族，每个族可分解为至多L次对角算符与固定秩二键算符的张量积，从而使每个族的指数成为O(n^L)个单式可控动量位移的精确乘积，且无族内Trotter误差。在d维N=2^n网格上，每Trotter步电路代价为O(d^{L+1}n^{L+2})个门。算法不依赖稀疏访问预言机或块编码，所有门由漂移多项式的系数以闭式固定。文中还证明了数值横坐标λ_max(H_1)的界，用于确定warped-phase变换的恢复域和后选择代价。两个非线性基准的经典模拟验证了结构定理、偏移恢复以及连续变量耦合相对离散模式寄存器的精度-资源优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性常微分方程在科学和工程领域普遍存在，而经典数值方法在高维情况下面临维度灾难。现有量子算法求解非线性ODE通常需要稀疏访问预言机或块编码等昂贵资源，且缺乏可扩展的结构化分解方法。本文旨在利用量子比特与连续变量混合平台的优势，通过代数结构分解实现对非线性ODE的高效量子模拟，同时避免对昂贵预言机资源的依赖。

Method: 采用混合qubit-qumode架构：首先通过Fokker-Planck方程将非线性ODE转化为概率密度演化问题；再利用Jin-Liu-Yu的warped-phase变换将离散生成元转化为带Fourier模参数的薛定谔方程族；将Fourier参数编码在单一连续变量量子模式上，空间离散采用d维网格。关键创新在于证明生成元厄米部分的二分Pauli分解结构，将Pauli串汇聚为O(log N)个互易交换族，每个族可因式分解为低次对角与秩二键算符的张量积，从而容许精确的乘积分解（无族内Trotter误差）。所有门由漂移多项式系数闭式确定。

Result: 电路复杂度为每Trotter步O(d^{L+1}n^{L+2})个门，其中n为每维网格位数。证明了数值横坐标上界以确定warped-phase恢复域和后选择代价。经典模拟在两个非线性基准上验证了结构定理、恢复特性以及连续变量耦合相对离散模式寄存器在精度-资源权衡上的优势。

Conclusion: 该算法展示了连续变量量子计算在非线性动力学模拟中的潜力，通过巧妙的代数结构分解避免了稀疏预言机与块编码的需求，为非线性ODE的量子模拟提供了一条可扩展、资源高效的路径。混合qubit-qumode框架和Pauli串排序分解是该工作的核心理论贡献。

Abstract: We develop a hybrid qubit--qumode algorithm for nonlinear ordinary differential equations of the form $\dot{\mathbf{x}}=\mathbf{f}(\mathbf{x})$ with drift of polynomial degree~$L$. Following the Fokker--Planck route of Tennie and Magri, the algorithm propagates the state density and returns the deterministic trajectory as the peak of that density in the small-noise limit. The discretised generator is carried into a parametrised family of Schrödinger equations by the warped-phase transformation of Jin, Liu, and Yu, and the Fourier-mode parameter of that family is placed on a single continuous-variable qumode. Our central structural result is that the Hermitian parts $H_{1}$ and $H_{2}$ of the discretised generator admit a bipartite Pauli decomposition that sorts the non-zero Pauli strings into $\mathcal{O}(\log N)$ mutually commuting families and factorises each family into a diagonal of degree at most $L$ tensored with a fixed rank-two bond operator. The factorisation renders each family exponential an exact product of $\mathcal{O}(n^{L})$ monomial-controlled momentum displacements, with no intra-family Trotter error. On a $d$-dimensional grid of $N=2^{n}$ points per axis the circuit costs $\mathcal{O}(d^{L+1}n^{L+2})$ gates per Trotter step. No sparse-access oracle and no block encoding is invoked: every gate is fixed in closed form by the polynomial coefficients of the drift. We also prove a bound on the numerical abscissa $λ_{\max}(H_{1})$ that fixes the recovery domain of the warped-phase transform and the post-selection cost. A classical simulation on two nonlinear benchmarks confirms the structural theorems, the shifted recovery, and the accuracy-per-resource advantage of the continuous-variable coupling over a discretised mode register.

</details>


### [171] [Statistically secure uncloneable encryption of arbitrary messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28561)
*Archishna Bhattacharyya,Anne Broadbent,Eric Culf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了基于Clifford酉子集编码的单比特不可克隆加密方案可被升级为任意长度消息的加密方案，实现统计安全的无条件不可克隆加密，且编码时间在消息长度和安全参数上都是多项式的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已实现了单比特的无条件不可克隆加密，但能否将其扩展到任意长度消息并实现统计安全性仍是未解问题。作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 利用已知安全的单比特方案中编码基由Clifford酉的一个子集构成这一关键事实，对方案进行升级改造，将其推广到任意长度消息的加密场景。

Result: 成功将单比特不可克隆加密方案升级为支持任意长度消息的方案，实现统计安全的无条件不可克隆加密，编码时间在消息长度和安全参数上均为多项式复杂度。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了一次性不可克隆加密对任意长度消息同样享有统计安全性，填补了该领域从单比特到任意长度消息之间的理论空白。

Abstract: Unconditional uncloneable encryption of a single bit with efficient encryption and decryption is now possible. However, whether the extension to messages of arbitrary length achieves statistical security remains to be known. Using the fact that the encoding bases for the single-bit scheme known to be secure consist of a subset of the Clifford unitaries, we show that this scheme can be upgraded to achieve unconditional uncloneable encryption for messages of arbitrary length, with encoding time polynomial in the message length and security parameter. This establishes that one-time uncloneable encryption of arbitrary messages enjoys statistical security.

</details>


### [172] [Benchmarking Quantum Simulations of the Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick Model Using Large Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28570)
*Maggie Bao,Rushil Dandamudi,Jerimiah Wright,Joan Étude Arrow,Henry Zou,Vardaan Sahgal,Brian J. McDermott*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）算法在NERSC Perlmutter超级计算机上计算Lipkin Meshkov Glick（LMG）模型的基态能量，生成了多达1400个粒子的基准数据集，并与IBM Eagle量子计算机上的VQE和SQD算法进行比较。结果表明，VQE在6个粒子以内误差小于1%，SQD将范围扩展到17个粒子，说明在含噪中等规模量子（NISQ）时代，子空间类方法能在精度、电路深度和噪声鲁棒性之间取得更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算逐渐成熟，需要将其实际问题求解性能与张量网络等经典竞争方法进行基准对比，以评估NISQ算法在真实硬件上的表现。

Method: 使用DMRG算法在Perlmutter超级计算机上计算LMG模型基态能量作为高精度经典基准，生成多达1400个粒子的数据集；随后在IBM Eagle量子计算机上运行VQE和SQD算法，将两者与DMRG结果进行对比。

Result: 提供了文献中规模最大的LMG基态能量数据集之一（至1400个粒子）。VQE在6个粒子时达到1%以内的误差，超出后则超过阈值；SQD将有效范围扩展至17个粒子。

Conclusion: 在NISQ时代，子空间类方法（如SQD）相对于变分方法（如VQE）展现出更好的精度与噪声鲁棒性平衡，是当前更具竞争力的量子算法路线。

Abstract: As quantum computing matures, it is critical to benchmark its real-world problem solving performance against competitive classical methods, such as tensor networks. In this work, we leverage the Density Matrix Renormalization Group (DMRG) algorithm to compute ground state energies of the Lipkin Meshkov Glick (LMG) model as a comparative benchmark against popular noisy intermediate-scale (NISQ) algorithms like the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) and Sample-Based Quantum Diagonalization (SQD) method. By running DMRG on the NERSC Perlmutter supercomputer, we provide one of the largest LMG ground state energy datasets in literature, containing accurate ground state energies for systems up to 1400 particles. We compare these results with VQE and SQD implementations on an IBM Eagle quantum computer for comparison. VQE achieved results within 1 percent error for 6 particles, while exceeding that threshold for all other values while SQD extended that range to 17 particles, suggesting that in a noisy intermediate scale quantum era, subspace-based approaches may strike the best balance between accuracy, circuit depth, and noise resilience.

</details>


### [173] [Quantum Fidelity-per-Cost: A Metric for Evaluation of Quantum Computing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28572)
*Siddarth Shinde,Jakub Szefer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨云计算提供商的量子计算机性价比比较方法，通过测量14个云QPU访问路径的电路执行保真度，并提出一个结合KL散度、射击数与货币成本的成本感知评分指标（QFC），实证发现成本感知排名与纯保真度排名存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算量子计算的普及使得硬件比较不仅是物理基准测试，更是实际的购买决策。然而，在异构计费模型下，成本感知的量子计算机比较仍未得到充分探索，用户难以进行价格敏感的硬件选择。

Method: （1）跨四个云访问路径（AWS、IBM Quantum Runtime、IQM Resonance、OQC）对14个云QPU访问路径（12个不同物理QPU）进行电路执行保真度的测量研究；（2）提出量子保真度-成本（QFC）评分指标，结合理想输出分布的KL散度、射击数与货币成本，并在明确文档化的计费模型下进行分析。

Result: 实证发现成本感知排名可能与纯保真度评估不同，用户在考虑价格时会选择不同的量子计算后端；该排名在指标重新加权下保持稳定；设备的计费模型而非硬件本身决定了其得分随射击数变化的规律；QFC值会随新机器上线或价格调整而变化。

Conclusion: 成本感知的量子计算机比较是可行且必要的，QFC提供了一种综合考虑保真度与成本的评估指标，帮助用户在异构计费模型下做出更明智的量子计算资源购买决策。

Abstract: Cloud-accessible quantum computing has made hardware comparison not only a physics benchmark but also a practical purchasing decision. Cost-aware comparison of quantum computers remains underexplored and is difficult to do under the heterogeneous billing models offered by various cloud-based quantum computing providers. This paper makes two main contributions to enable price-aware comparison of quantum computers. First, this work presents a cross-provider measurement study of quantum circuit execution fidelity spanning 14 cloud QPU access-path entries (12 distinct physical QPUs) across four cloud access paths: Amazon Web Services (AWS) cloud, IBM Quantum Runtime (IBM) cloud, IQM Resonance (IQM) cloud, and Oxford Quantum Circuits (OQC) cloud. Second, this work proposes and analyzes a cost-aware score, Quantum Fidelity-per-Cost (QFC), which combines Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence from an ideal output distribution, shot count, and monetary cost into one possible metric under a documented billing model. The main empirical observation from this work is that cost-aware ranking can differ from purely fidelity-based evaluation of quantum computers, and that users may select different quantum computing backends when they consider price in their selection, as opposed to selection based on fidelity alone. This work shows that the ranking is stable under reweighting of the metric, and that a device's billing model, not its hardware, governs how its score scales with shot count. Reported QFC values change as new machines come online or as providers revise their prices.

</details>


### [174] [Explicit Matrices over $\mathbb Z_2$ with CNOT and Row Complexity $4n-\mathrm{o}(n)$ and Local Logic Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28598)
*Sherry Gong,Andrew Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一族显式的可逆 $\mathbb Z_2$ 上的 $n\times n$ 矩阵，其 CNOT 复杂度和行化简复杂度下界为 $4n-\text{o}(n)$；同时证明由局部线性逻辑门生成的置换群 $G_n$ 同构于 $\mathbb Z_2^n$ 上所有可逆仿射变换构成的群，从而将量子复杂度估计问题转化为矩阵的行化简复杂度问题，并由此得到相关置换的量子复杂度下界也为 $4n-\text{o}(n)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算中，量化量子线路（特别是由 CNOT 门或局部线性门构成的线路）的复杂度下界是一个核心难题。本文旨在通过建立置换群与可逆仿射变换群之间的同构关系，将量子复杂度问题归约到经典的矩阵行化简复杂度问题，从而获得显式的线性复杂度下界。

Method: (1) 构造一族显式的可逆 $n\times n$ 矩阵（基于 $\mathbb Z_2$），证明其 CNOT 复杂度和行化简复杂度下界为 $4n-\text{o}(n)$；(2) 证明局部线性逻辑门生成的置换群 $G_n$ 自然同构于 $\mathbb Z_2^n$ 上所有可逆仿射变换构成的群，从而将置换的量子复杂度问题归约为矩阵的行化简复杂度问题；(3) 利用上述同构，将矩阵复杂度下界移植为对应置换的量子复杂度下界。

Result: 给出了复杂度下界为 $4n-\text{o}(n)$ 的显式可逆矩阵族，且该下界在更强的计算模型（任意作用在坐标对上的可逆线性变换替代 CNOT 门）下仍然成立；进一步通过群同构证明对应置换的量子复杂度下界同样为 $4n-\text{o}(n)$。

Conclusion: 本文通过代数群论的方法，将量子线路（局部线性门）的复杂度下界问题与经典线性代数中的行化简复杂度问题建立联系，获得了线性量级（$4n-\text{o}(n)$）的显式下界，为量子复杂度下界研究提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: In this article, we present an explicit family of invertible $n\times n$ matrices over $\mathbb Z_2$ whose CNOT and row complexity is at least $4n-\text{o}(n)$; equivalently, reducing these matrices to the identity requires at least $4n-\text{o}(n)$ elementary row operations. Moreover, the same complexity lower bound holds in the stronger computational model where the CNOT gates are replaced by arbitrary local linear logic gates, namely arbitrary invertible linear transformations acting on pairs of coordinates.
  Let $G_n$ denote the permutation group generated by local logic gates acting on the set of binary strings of length $n$. We prove that $G_n$ is naturally isomorphic to the group of all invertible affine transformations of the vector space $\mathbb Z_2^n$, thus reducing the problem of estimating the quantum complexity of permutations in $G_n$ to the row reduction complexity of invertible matrices over $\mathbb Z_2$. As an application, we show that the permutations associated with our explicit matrices have quantum complexity at least $4n-\text{o}(n)$.

</details>


### [175] [SymFT: Universal Fault-Tolerant Quantum Circuit Simulation via Symbolic Clifford--Pauli Frames and Stabilizer Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28600)
*Wang Fang,Huazhe Lou,Riling Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SymFT是一个面向Clifford主导电路的高吞吐量模拟器，支持Pauli旋转、随机Pauli噪声、中途测量及测量记录控制的反馈。通过符号Clifford-Pauli帧分解将分支概率采样简化为Pauli旋转和测量投影，并结合自适应stabilizer坐标规划，避免逐次采样中的tableau更新，在纯Clifford和近Clifford电路上实现了最先进的采样性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错协议大多由stabilizer子电路构成，但实现通用性所需的非Clifford操作使得精确采样计算成本高昂，需要高效的模拟器来加速Clifford主导电路的采样。

Method: 结合两个核心思想：(1) 符号Clifford-Pauli帧分解——将分支概率采样化简为Pauli旋转和测量投影，噪声和反馈以符号表示，残差单元部分不需在每shot中应用；(2) 自适应stabilizer坐标规划——使用共享的stabilizer-destabilizer tableau定义基，仅存储活跃的非stabilizer自由度于动态大小的稠密态向量中，提前解析基变换并生成直接的多坐标采样指令。

Result: 在单CPU核心上，SymFT在surface-code电路上比Stim快2.51-2.56倍，在magic-state培养和蒸馏电路上比Clifft快1.86-3.51倍；对于培养电路，采样吞吐量比先前模拟器SOFT高出两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: SymFT通过避免逐shot的tableau更新和局域化引起的Clifford变换，在全Clifford和近Clifford电路上实现了最先进的采样性能，为容错量子协议的高吞吐量模拟提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant protocols often consist largely of stabilizer subcircuits, yet the non-Clifford operations required for universality make exact sampling costly. We present SymFT, a high-throughput simulator for Clifford-dominated circuits with Pauli rotations, stochastic Pauli noise, mid-circuit Pauli measurements, and measurement-record-controlled Pauli feedback. It combines two ideas. First, symbolic Clifford--Pauli frame factorization reduces branch-probability sampling to Pauli rotations and measurement projectors, with noise and feedback represented by symbolic signs. Since the residual Clifford and Pauli frames are unitary, they do not affect branch probabilities and need not be applied in every shot. Second, adaptive stabilizer-coordinate planning uses a shared stabilizer--destabilizer tableau to define the basis and stores only the active non-stabilizer degrees of freedom in a dynamically sized dense active-state vector. It resolves basis changes once and emits direct multi-coordinate sampling instructions, thereby avoiding per-shot tableau updates and localization-induced Clifford transformations of the dense vector. Across the tested pure-Clifford and near-Clifford circuits, SymFT achieves state-of-the-art sampling performance. On a single CPU core, it is $2.51\text{--}2.56\times$ faster than Stim for surface-code circuits and $1.86\text{--}3.51\times$ faster than Clifft for magic-state cultivation and distillation circuits. For the tested cultivation circuits, its sampling throughput also exceeds that of our previous simulator, SOFT, by more than two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [176] [Pauli Encodings & Unclonable Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28602)
*Pierre Botteron,Sébastien Designolle,Omar Fawzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了"不可克隆比特"问题，引入并分析了一类比特加密方案——Pauli编码。作者证明了最优单配性纠缠获胜概率的通用下界，揭示了自然方法的局限性（X/Z字符串限制和基于成对猜测边缘的方法），并在特定协议下解决了半定规划的第三层松弛，同时证明了多种Pauli族满足强不可克隆-不可区分安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子加密是否能够阻止敌人在密钥泄露后产生两个都能揭示明文的系统，这一"不可克隆比特"问题是量子密码学的核心开放问题之一。作者希望通过引入新的加密方案类（Pauli编码）并对其安全性进行系统分析，推进对该问题的理解。

Method: 引入Pauli编码这类加密方案（密文为Pauli串的归一化本征空间投影算子），利用单配性纠缠框架分析其安全性；推导通用下界及若干结构族的更紧界；针对两反交换协议，利用对称性求解自然半定规划松弛的第三层；对限维敌手和高效Pauli族证明强不可克隆-不可区分安全性，并结合NPA计算提供数值证据。

Result: 证明了每个含K个Pauli串的Pauli编码的最优单配性纠缠获胜概率下界为1/2+1/(2√K)；证明限制为X和Z字符串的编码不安全；发现3/4通用障碍表明仅靠成对猜测边缘无法证明不可克隆-不可区分安全；对两两反交换协议解出第三层SDP松弛，获胜概率渐近上界约0.5556；证明多个高效Pauli族的强不可克隆-不可区分安全性。

Conclusion: Pauli编码为研究不可克隆比特提供了一个富有成效的框架。尽管存在自然方法的局限（X/Z限制不安全、成对边缘不够），该工作通过对称性分析和SDP松弛取得了显著的进展，且对限维敌手及若干高效Pauli族的安全性证明为最终解决不可克隆比特问题奠定了基础。

Abstract: The unclonable bit question asks whether quantum encryption can prevent an adversary from producing two systems that both reveal the plaintext once the key is disclosed. We introduce and study Pauli Encodings, a simple class of one-bit encryption schemes whose ciphertexts are normalized eigenspace projectors of Pauli strings. For every Pauli Encoding with K Pauli strings, we prove a universal lower bound $1/2+1/(2\sqrt{K})$ on the optimal monogamy-of-entanglement winning probability, together with sharper bounds for several structured families. We then establish two limitations of natural approaches to unclonable security. First, if the Pauli strings are restricted to strings of X and Z of length n, the encoding is not secure. Second, we identify a universal 3/4 obstruction showing that arguments based only on pairwise guessing marginals cannot establish unclonable-indistinguishable security. When the Pauli strings all pairwise anticommute, the protocol becomes the one studied in [Quantum 10, 2157 (2026)]. We exploit the symmetry of this protocol to solve the third level of the natural semidefinite programming relaxation obtaining an asymptotic upper bound of approximately 0.5556 on the winning probability. Finally, we prove strong unclonable-indistinguishable security against bounded-local-dimension adversaries and strong indistinguishability security for several efficient Pauli families. First-level NPA computations provide additional numerical evidence towards the strong unclonable-indistinguishable security.

</details>


### [177] [Logical computation with canonical lifted product codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28605)
*Han Zheng,Guo Zheng,Liang Jiang,Qian Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过协同设计规范提升积（LP）量子LDPC码及其逻辑指令集，利用循环对称性构建规范逻辑基，实现包括常深度自动态/折叠横向量子门、模块化图码手术、高并行逻辑测量和并行魔术态注入在内的完整逻辑指令集，推进超高码率容错量子计算的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高码率量子LDPC码能以较低的物理量子比特开销编码大量逻辑量子比特，但在这种稠密编码上实现高效的容错量子计算仍然是一个重大挑战。现有的通用技术（如码手术和门传送）虽然适用范围广，但在结构未被利用的复杂高码率编码上难以实现模块化、低开销和完全可验证的操作。

Method: 作者采用代码与逻辑指令集协同设计的策略，针对具有循环对称性的规范提升积（LP）码族，证明这类码存在规范逻辑基，其中共轭逻辑算子按行和列组织为从底层经典码继承的循环轨道。基于此规范基，实现了一系列逻辑操作：常深度自同构和折叠横向量子门、由常数个可复用种子手术器件或紧凑规范提取器构建的模块化图码手术、高度并行的逻辑泡利乘积测量，以及并行魔术态注入。

Result: 以[[1122,148,≤20]]（resp. [[4350,1224,≤20]]）LP码为例，分别仅需两个（resp. 四个）种子手术器件；任意高权重逻辑测量可通过比数据码块一半还小的完整提取器实现。

Conclusion: 这些结果推进了超高码率量子架构上容错量子计算的前沿，展示了高码率LP码不仅可用于存储，还能支持高效、模块化的逻辑操作。

Abstract: High-rate quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes encode many logical qubits with low physical-qubit overhead, but realizing efficient fault-tolerant computation on such dense encodings remains a major challenge. Generic, code-agnostic techniques such as code surgery and gate teleportation apply broadly, but are difficult to make modular, low-overhead, and fully certifiable on complex high-rate codes whose structure is left unexploited. Here we overcome these obstacles by co-designing the code together with its logical instruction set for a broad family of \emph{canonical} lifted-product (LP) codes with cyclic symmetry. We show that these codes admit a \emph{canonical logical basis}, in which conjugate logical operators are organized into rows and columns of cyclic orbits inherited directly from the underlying classical codes, analogous to the structure that makes hypergraph-product codes so tractable. This canonical basis unlocks a complete logical instruction set, including constant-depth automorphism and fold-transversal Clifford gates, modular graph code surgeries built from a constant number of reusable seed surgery gadgets or a compact canonical extractor, highly parallel logical Pauli-product measurements, and parallel magic-state injection. For example, a $[[1122,148,\leq\!20]]$ (resp. $[[4350,1224,\leq\!20]]$) LP code requires only two (resp. four) seed surgery gadgets, while arbitrary high-weight logical measurements can be implemented using a full extractor smaller than half of the data code block. These results advance the frontier of fault-tolerant quantum computation on ultra-high-rate quantum architectures.

</details>


### [178] [Learning Arbitrary Lindbladians from Time Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28610)
*Zhili Chen,Zhan Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效算法，用于从未知的马尔可夫开放量子系统的时间演化中学习其Lindbladian生成器（包含哈密顿项和耗散项）。算法通过两个非自适应、无辅助比特、无控制的阶段（支持学习阶段和系数学习阶段），以近乎最优的实验次数和总演化时间（匹配下界至对数因子）学习任意Lindbladian的全部系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习未知开放量子系统的动力学（即Lindbladian生成器）是量子系统辨识和量子计算中的基础问题。Lindbladian包含由指数级规模的Pauli项索引的哈密顿和耗散系数，直接学习面临维度灾难。需要一个在温和假设下高效、可扩展且接近最优的算法来恢复任意Lindbladian的全部系数。

Method: 算法由两个非自适应、无ancilla、无控制阶段组成：(1) 支持学习阶段：制备乘积Pauli本征态并进行单比特Pauli测量，以$\tilde O(\Lambda^2/\eta^2)$次实验输出一个大小为poly($\Lambda/\eta$)的候选支持集，包含所有幅度不小于$\eta$的哈密顿和耗散坐标；(2) 系数学习阶段：制备随机稳定子态并在随机Clifford基下测量，以$\tilde O(\Lambda^2\log M/\epsilon^2)$次实验估计候选支持中所有$M$个系数至误差$\epsilon$。两个阶段组合实现多项式时间内对任意Lindbladian的完全辨识。

Result: 算法以$\tilde O(\Lambda^2/\epsilon^2)$次实验和$\tilde O(\Lambda/\epsilon^2)$总演化时间学习任意Lindbladian，同时保持多项式经典计算复杂度。实验次数的标度和总演化时间分别匹配信息论下界（至多对数因子），表明算法对学习任意Lindbladian是近乎最优的。

Conclusion: 该工作首次给出了在温和假设下学习任意Lindbladian的近乎最优算法，其无自适应、无辅助比特、无控制的设计使其具有实践可行性。实验次数和总演化时间均达到最优标度，为开放量子系统的量子过程层析提供了本质上最优的解决方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning an unknown Markovian open-system generator from access to its physical time evolution. This generator, called a Lindbladian, contains Hamiltonian and dissipative coefficients indexed by an exponentially large family of possible Pauli terms. We propose an efficient algorithm that learns arbitrary Lindbladians from time evolution under minimal assumptions. For a Lindbladian of dynamical strength at most $Λ$, the algorithm estimates every coefficient to error $ε$ using $\widetilde O(Λ^2/ε^2)$ experiments and $\widetilde O(Λ/ε^2)$ total evolution time, together with polynomial classical running time. The algorithm consists of two nonadaptive, ancilla-free, and control-free stages: 1. The support-learning stage outputs a candidate support of size $\mathrm{poly}(Λ/η)$ that contains every Hamiltonian and dissipative coordinate of magnitude at least $η$, using $\widetilde O(Λ^2/η^2)$ experiments with preparations of product Pauli eigenstates and single-qubit Pauli measurements. 2.The coefficient-learning stage estimates all coefficients in any candidate support of size $M$ to error $ε$, using $\widetilde O(Λ^2\log M/ε^{2})$ experiments with preparations of random stabilizer states and measurements in random Clifford bases. Composing the two stages identifies and estimates every coefficient of an arbitrary Lindbladian in polynomial time. The experiment-count and total-evolution-time scalings match the lower bounds up to logarithmic factors, so the algorithm is nearly optimal for learning arbitrary Lindbladians.

</details>


### [179] [On Optimal Measurement-State Preparation via Geometric Transport of the Squeezing Ellipse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28620)
*Vsevolod Salakhutdinov,Nikolay Kalinin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个几何框架，用于在SU(2)对称系统中将初始失准的压缩态转换为测量最优态。框架中压缩椭圆的取向作为额外几何自由度，随平均态在单位球面上的轨迹演化，其旋转由路径几何决定，并与固相角（几何相位）建立联系，适用于极化压缩光等多种物理平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子计量中，制备最优测量态是利用压缩态的关键前提。然而初始压缩输入往往存在失准问题，需要建立统一的几何框架来理解和实现失准压缩态向测量最优态的转换，并为不同物理平台提供通用指导。

Method: 采用几何框架方法：将平均态控制在单位球面上的轨迹运动，同时将压缩椭圆的取向视为额外的几何自由度。通过分析轨迹路径的几何特性（特别是所张立体角）来确定椭圆旋转，并与几何相位建立联系。以偏振压缩光为例，提出利用连续变化的双折射元件来实现该框架的可行性方案。

Result: 建立了压缩椭圆取向与平均态路径几何之间的定量联系，证明对于相关变换类别，椭圆旋转取决于轨迹所张的立体角，建立了与几何相位的关联。该框架具有通用性，适用于不同物理平台，并在偏振压缩光系统中给出了具体实现方案。

Conclusion: 该几何框架为压缩态测量最优化的制备提供了统一且直观的理解途径，将物理平台无关的几何结构与可实现的实验方案相结合，为量子计量中压缩态的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Preparation of optimal measurement states is a key requirement in quantum metrology utilizing squeezed states. We discuss a geometric framework that transforms an initially misaligned squeezed input into a measurement-optimal state in SU(2)-symmetric systems. Within this framework, the orientation of the squeezing ellipse constitutes an additional geometric degree of freedom and evolves as the mean state follows a controlled trajectory on the unit sphere. The resulting rotation of the ellipse is determined by the geometry of the path and, for the relevant class of transformations, depends on the solid angle enclosed by the trajectory, establishing a connection with the geometric phase. The discussed framework is applicable to different physical platforms. As a particular example, we consider polarization-squeezed light and outline a possible implementation using a continuously varying birefringent element.

</details>
