### [180] [An infinitesimal deformation of the post-Lie and post-Hopf algebra correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28009)
*Andrea Rivezzi,Andrea Sciandra,Thomas Weber*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究了post-Lie代数与post-Hopf代数的无穷小形变理论，证明普遍包络代数与原始元素函子构成的伴随与无穷小结构相容；在连通余交换无穷小post-Hopf代数情形下给出范畴等价，推广了Cartier–Milnor–Moore定理。同时完成了sl(2)上的无穷小post-Lie结构分类、Sweedler Hopf代数上的无穷小post-Hopf结构分类，并证明无穷小post-Lie代数的二次操作元是Koszul的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Cartier–Milnor–Moore定理（连通余交换Hopf代数与其原始元素Lie代数的等价）推广到具有无穷小形变结构的post-Lie与post-Hopf代数范畴中，并系统研究此类新代数结构的分类与操作元性质。

Method: 通过定义无穷小post-Lie代数与无穷小post-Hopf代数，利用普遍包络代数和原始元素函子建立伴随对应；借助范畴等价论证推广CMM定理；通过具体计算分类sl(2)上的全部无穷小post-Lie结构及Sweedler Hopf代数上的无穷小post-Hopf结构；利用李代数操作元与双拟群操作元之间的滤过分配律证明二次操作元的Koszul性；还建立了无穷小post-Hopf代数与Hochschild 2-上循环之间的联系。

Result: 证明了伴随函子与无穷小结构相容，并在连通余交换情形下得到范畴等价（CMM定理的推广）；完成了sl(2)上无穷小post-Lie结构的完全分类，处理了具有协变常数挠率的平坦联络相关的一类例子；完成了Sweedler Hopf代数上无穷小post-Hopf结构的分类；证明了余交换无穷小post-Hopf代数诱导子伴随Hopf代数上的Hochschild 2-上循环；最终用滤过分配律证明了无穷小post-Lie代数操作元的Koszul性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了无穷小post-Lie与post-Hopf代数系统的形变理论框架，将经典Hopf代数结构定理推广至更一般的post-范畴，并在分类与操作元理论两方面提供了完整结果，为该领域的后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We describe infinitesimal deformations of post-Lie algebras and post-Hopf algebras and prove that the adjunction given by the universal enveloping algebra and primitive elements functors is compatible with the infinitesimal structure. When restricted to connected and cocommutative infinitesimal post-Hopf algebras, this becomes an equivalence of categories, which constitutes an extension of the Cartier--Milnor--Moore theorem. We classify infinitesimal post-Lie structures on $\mathfrak{sl}(2)$, and discuss a class of infinitesimal post-Lie algebras emerging from flat connections with covariantly-constant torsion. Moreover, we classify infinitesimal post-Hopf structures on Sweedler's Hopf algebra. Cocommutative infinitesimal post-Hopf algebras induce a Hochschild 2-cocycle on the associated subadjacent Hopf algebra. Finally, we prove that the quadratic operad of infinitesimal post-Lie algebras is Koszul, by using a filtered distributive law between the operads of Lie algebras and bi-magmas.

</details>


### [181] [Double shuffle relations imply the infinitesimal hexagon equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28163)
*Muze Ren*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文证明了双重shuffle李代数𝔡𝔪𝔯₀中的任意元素ψ满足无穷小六边形方程[ψ(x,y),x]+[ψ(−x−y,y),−x−y]=0，证明方法是通过比较两个不同的Hopf代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重zeta值（MZV）是数论中的重要研究对象，而Double shuffle李代数𝔡𝔪𝔯₀是Racinet在多重zeta值的代数研究中引入的关键代数结构。证明𝔡𝔪𝔯₀中元素满足无穷小六边形方程，有助于深化对这一代数结构及其在量子代数/辫子群中作用的理解。

Method: 作者通过构造并比较两个不同的Hopf代数来建立证明。通过考察这两个Hopf代数之间的关系，利用两者的代数结构差异来推导出𝔡𝔪𝔯₀中任意元素ψ必须满足无穷小六边形方程。

Result: 对于任意ψ∈𝔡𝔪𝔯₀，证明了其满足无穷小六边形方程[ψ(x,y),x]+[ψ(−x−y,y),−x−y]=0。

Conclusion: 无穷小六边形方程是𝔡𝔪𝔯₀中元素的固有性质，可通过两个不同Hopf代数的比较来证得，这为理解Double shuffle李代数的结构提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Double shuffle Lie algebra $\mathfrak{dmr}_0$ was introduced by G.~Racinet in the algebraic study of the multiple zeta values. In this note, we prove that for any $ψ\in \mathfrak{dmr}_0$, it satisfies the infinitesimal hexagon equation $[ψ(x,y),x]+[ψ(-x-y,y),-x-y]=0$. The proof is through the comparison of two different Hopf algebras.

</details>


### [182] [The structure of iterated Hopf Ore extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28234)
*B. -F. Yu,G. -S. Zhou*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究特征零域上迭代Hopf Ore扩张（IHOE），证明了该类Hopf代数对Hopf子代数与商Hopf代数是封闭的，且其单侧余理想子代数均为迭代Ore扩张。基于PBW生成系统的继承定理和thin替代机制，作者开发了枚举所有非平凡单侧余理想子代数的组合算法，并完整分类了GK维数为三的非余交换连通Hopf代数的非平凡右余理想子代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IHOE是Gelfand-Kirillov维数有限的连通Hopf代数中的重要类，但对其子代数结构和余理想子代数的系统性理解尚不充分。理解这些结构性质对于分类低维连通Hopf代数及其子对象至关重要。

Method: 基于Kharchenko的thin替代机制处理PBW生成系统；证明PBW生成系统的继承定理作为从环境代数向子代数传递PBW生成系统的系统扰动方法；利用这些理论结果设计显式组合算法枚举非平凡单侧余理想子代数。

Result: (1) IHOE类对Hopf子代数和商Hopf代数封闭；(2) IHOE的每个单侧余理想子代数都是迭代Ore扩张；(3) 给出一般PBW生成系统继承定理；(4) 开发显式组合枚举算法；(5) 完成GK维数三的非余交换连通Hopf代数中所有非平凡右余理想子代数的显式分类。

Conclusion: 本文为IHOE的子代数结构提供了完整的理论基础和计算工具，加深了对有限GK维连通Hopf代数余理想子代数的理解，并为后续研究更高维情形提供了系统性方法。

Abstract: This paper studies iterated Hopf Ore extensions (IHOEs) over a field $\Bbbk$ of characteristic zero, a significant class of connected Hopf algebras with finite Gelfand-Kirillov dimension. We establish two fundamental structural properties for arbitrary IHOEs of $\Bbbk$. First, the class of IHOEs of $\Bbbk$ is closed under Hopf subalgebras and quotient Hopf algebras. Second, every one-sided coideal subalgebra of an IHOE of $\Bbbk$ is an iterated Ore extension of $\Bbbk$. These structural facts are built upon the thin replacement machinery for PBW generating systems originating from Kharchenko's work. We further prove a general inheritance theorem for PBW generating systems, which serves as a systematic perturbation method to produce PBW generating systems for subalgebras from that of the ambient algebra under mild hypotheses. Based on these theoretical advances, we develop an explicit combinatorial algorithm for enumerating all nontrivial one-sided coideal subalgebras of arbitrary IHOEs of $\Bbbk$. As practical illustrations, we explicitly classify all nontrivial right coideal subalgebras of noncocommutative connected Hopf algebras of GK-dimension three.

</details>
